| | | | |
| | | | |
| | | | |
| | | | |
| | | | |

> [!cn-theory]
> 错因六类：介词搭配错误 / 修饰依附不清 / 名词关系不清 / 必要信息遗漏 / 表达冗长 / 时空错位。同一错因出现 3 次以上，需回到教学文档对应章节复习。

---

<h1 id="sec-4">第4篇：写作编码：句子、语篇与高级修订专项</h1>

> [!cn-theory]
> **对应理论**：理论全书第4篇【写作编码：句子、语篇与修订】。
> 本篇涵盖写作核心模型 14 组，以及基础句子组装、四大模型高阶压缩、名词化与 7 大关系动词、信息焦点倒装、段落衔接与四大主位推进、定从修订、识解与焦点拓展、五步造句、十大红线修正与 Swales CARS 语步分析，共 190 题（另含核心模型 14 组）。

---

## 4.1 训练方法与评分流程

### 4.1.1 建议流程

1. **裸写**：不查答案，按题目限制完成第一稿。
2. **自检**：圈出主语和谓语，给关系词加框，给新增修饰语画下划线。
3. **核对**：对照文末示范答案，只比较结构、逻辑和信息层级，不机械背诵。
4. **二改**：合上答案，在不看原句的情况下重新写一遍。
5. **回译**：24 小时后只看中文信息，再次输出英文。

### 4.1.2 句子题评分（每题 10 分）

- 语法准确：4 分
- 信息完整、语义不走样：3 分
- 信息层级与焦点清楚：2 分
- 表达简洁自然：1 分

### 4.1.3 段落与篇章题评分

- 内容完成度：25%
- 逻辑与连贯：25%
- 语法准确度：25%
- 词汇与句式质量：15%
- 文体、格式与语气：10%

### 4.1.4 每次训练记录

> [!cn-practice]
> 日期：  
> 题号：  
> 首稿用时：  
> 首稿得分：  
> 错因：主语中心误判 / 动词关系读反 / 修饰对象找错 / 范围泛化 / 过度推断 / 主谓脱节 / 其他  
> 二改句：  
> 24 小时回测：通过 / 未通过  

---

## 4.2 写作核心模型训练（14 组）

### 4.2.1 题干与作答

**作答前先看不同内容的示范**：把“Maya 在家”放到不同位置。

- 作主语：That Maya is at home reassures Leo.（Maya 在家这件事让 Leo 放心。）
- 作宾语：Leo knows that Maya is at home.（Leo 知道 Maya 在家。）
- 作表语：The good news is that Maya is at home.（好消息是 Maya 在家。）
- 说明名词内容：The news that Maya is at home reassures Leo.（Maya 在家的消息让 Leo 放心。）

圈出 that 从句，标出作用。其他组也先看具体变化：The plan failed, which caused delays. → The failure of the plan caused delays. 这两句都说“计划失败导致延误”；If Maya arrives late, we will wait. → In the event of Maya arriving late, we will wait. 这两句都保留“如果 Maya 迟到”这一条件。示范为自编材料，不是下列各题的答案。

与理论全书第4篇同名练习组对应；先独立完成下面 14 组核心模型训练，完成后翻至本篇末尾 **4.12.1 节** 核对参考答案与认知精解。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-1"></div>


**写作组 1 · GPS 时空定位**
把下面四条信息合成为一句，保留一个主句，并完整交代时间、地点和目的：
`The committee announced the decision.` / `The announcement took place yesterday.` / `The venue was the conference hall.` / `The purpose was to address the budget deficit.`
自检要点：三类信息是否保留，说明分别接在哪一部分，读起来是否自然；不把句末必须最长作为合格条件。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-1" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-2"></div>


**写作组 2 · 定语从句：限定对象还是补充信息**
根据括号中的语境，填写关系词，补上需要的逗号，并说明从句用于限定对象还是补充信息：
`The report ______ was submitted last month contained serious errors.`（有多份报告，用提交时间辨明是哪份）
`The 2023 report ______ was submitted last month contained serious errors.`（双方已明确所指的2023年报告，提交时间是补充说明）
自检要点：从句是否用来辨明所指报告；补充性从句的标点与关系词是否匹配。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-2" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-3"></div>


**写作组 3 · 把一整件事放进名词性从句**
把“Algorithmic bias may distort credit scoring.”这段意思分别作主语、宾语、表语，或放在名词后说明内容，写四个完整句子。圈出相应从句并标作用，保留 may 表示的可能性。
自检要点：主语较长时是否改用 It 形式主语；名词后的内容说明位置前是否有抽象锚点名词（risk / concern / evidence）。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-3" data-lines="12" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-4"></div>


**写作组 4 · 先说让步背景，还是先说决定**
同一组信息写出前置版与后置版，说明两版先交代什么；焦点如需进一步判断，应另给上下文或重音线索：
`尽管初步数据支持药效，监管机构仍要求更广泛的临床试验。`
自检要点：前置版是否需要逗号；后置版是否根据语义选择逗号；结合语境说明两版的信息安排，不强行指定某个词为焦点。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-4" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-5"></div>


**写作组 5 · 介词修饰链组装**
用修饰语交代下列核心名词的具体对象、地域与时间，并判断各短语的依附关系：
核心词 `the decline`（具体化为“过去十年东南亚各经济体制造业产出的下降”）
自检要点：各介词短语所修饰的对象是否明确；若作为普通单数主语使用，谓语是否与 `decline` 一致。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-5" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-6"></div>


**写作组 6 · 对称分叉改写**
把两个并列从句合并为一个带分叉介词链的单句：
`Monetary tightening hurts small firms. Persistent inflation also hurts household consumption.`
自检要点：两个分支是否共用同一中心名词（如 `effects`）；`of A on X and of B on Y` 的对应关系是否错位。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-6" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-7"></div>


**写作组 7 · 介词后接带主体的 -ing 结构**
把从句改写为"介词 + 逻辑主语 + 动名词"结构：
`If regulators fail to publish the stress-test results, market confidence will erode.`
自检要点：动名词的逻辑主语是否写出（`the regulators failing to...`）；改写后主句是否仍然完整。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-7" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-8"></div>


**写作组 8 · 动词表达 → 名词化表达**
把下面这句改写为名词化高密度版本，去掉所有 because / so：
`Because the pathogen mutated rapidly, the vaccine became much less effective, so researchers had to redesign the protocol.`
自检要点：是否用名词准确表达两个过程；所选动词是否符合原关系。precipitate 表示促使某事突然或迅速发生，necessitate 表示使某事成为必要，二者不能任意互换。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-8" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-9"></div>


**写作组 9 · 冗余表达与动词选择**
改写：`The occurrence of the crisis was a cause of serious problems for the economy.`
自检要点：是否减少冗余表达，并根据原意选择动词；be / cause 本身没有错误，不能为替换而改变意义。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-9" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-10"></div>


**写作组 10 · 总结性同位语替换 which**
改写：`The central bank injected massive liquidity, which was a strategy that raised inflation concerns.`
自检要点：是否用"概括性抽象名词 + that 从句"（a maneuver that...）替代定语从句。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-10" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-11"></div>


**写作组 11 · 恢复性同位语接力**
在下面长句后重复关键名词，再围绕它补充一层新说明：
`The committee proposed a sweeping reform of executive compensation, ...`
自检要点：重复的名词是否正是全句议题；第二层展开是否提供了新的判断而非复述。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-11" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-12"></div>


**写作组 12 · 分配式绝对短语**
用 `each + V-ing` 把下列信息收束到句末：
`五位上诉法官各自给出了推翻判例的不同法理依据。`
自检要点：绝对短语是否有独立逻辑主语；句末补充说明是否清楚。不能只凭位置判定信息焦点。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-12" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-13"></div>


**写作组 13**：把下面这句改为学术讨论与高考建议两个版本（均按证据和目的调整立场强度）：`Social media damages teenagers' mental health.`

未提供真实研究证据，本题只练习可能性判断与建议表达，不引用虚构研究来支持结论。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-13" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Core-14"></div>


**写作组 14**：自选一篇论述性短文，找一处衔接不清和一处结尾作用不清的位置。按需要修改，并说明是在概括、解释还是过渡；不要求每段都使用 This 或把重点放在末句。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Core-14" data-lines="12" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

## 4.3 基础句子组装与高阶压缩进阶（第 1—44 题）

### 4.3.1 GPS 场景定位（第 1—8 题）

**SVO 示例**：Maya（主语 S）read（谓语动词 V）the report（宾语 O），意思是“Maya 读了报告”。本组先保留给定结构，再补题目所需说明；不要求所有英文句子都用 SVO。

> 对应理论：4.2.1节。

要求：保留给出的主语 + 谓语动词 + 宾语（SVO）主干，把时间、地点、方式、状态或目的安放到合适位置。不要把所有信息堆在名词前。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q1"></div>


1. 主干：`The school will launch a reading campaign.`  
信息：下周一上午；在礼堂；为了鼓励学生每天阅读三十分钟。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q1" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q2"></div>


2. 主干：`The volunteers planted young trees.`  
信息：早春；沿河岸；在当地居民的帮助下；为了防止水土流失。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q2" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q3"></div>


3. 主干：`Dr. Chen presented the findings.`  
信息：经过两年实地研究；在北京的一次国际会议上；向来自十五个国家的专家。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q3" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q4"></div>


4. 主干：`The rescue team found the hikers.`  
信息：黎明前；在一间废弃木屋附近；借助热成像设备。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q4" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q5"></div>


5. 主干：`Our class held a charity sale.`  
信息：午休期间；在操场上；为了给乡村学校购买体育器材。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q5" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q6"></div>


6. 主干：`Mia opened the package.`  
信息：生日当天早晨；坐在厨房餐桌旁；双手因期待而微微颤抖。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q6" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q7"></div>


7. 主干：`The committee announced its decision.`  
信息：经过三个小时的激烈讨论；在一次线上会议上；回应公众对数据隐私的担忧。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q7" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q8"></div>


8. 主干：`The museum will display the letters.`  
信息：从下个月开始；在西侧展厅；修复完成后首次公开展出。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q8" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.3.2 定语从句信息嫁接（第 9—16 题）

> 对应理论：4.2.2节。

要求：合并或改写句子，并根据语义选择限定性或非限制性定语从句。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q9"></div>


9. The students returned their consent forms before Friday. The students may join the field trip.  
限定范围：只有按时交表的学生可以参加。

须保留“只有”的排他含义；仅使用限定性定语从句不能自动表达这一含义。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q9" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q10"></div>


10. Ms. Patel will give the opening speech. Ms. Patel founded the community science club ten years ago.  
人物身份已明确，后句为补充信息。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q10" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q11"></div>


11. The library extended its weekend opening hours. This allowed more working parents to visit with their children.  
要求：用修饰整个前句的 `which`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q11" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q12"></div>


12. We visited a wetland park. More than two hundred bird species have been recorded there.  
要求：使用 `where`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q12" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q13"></div>


13. I still remember the afternoon. Our debate team won its first regional championship then.  
要求：使用 `when`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q13" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q14"></div>


14. The school created an online platform. Through the platform, students can book individual meetings with teachers.  
要求：使用“介词 + 关系代词”。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q14" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q15"></div>


15. The artist donated twelve paintings to the town. Her childhood was spent in the town.  
要求：使用 `whose`，且不要改变“艺术家在这里度过童年”的意思。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q15" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q16"></div>


16. 原句存在锚点歧义：  
`The editor interviewed the assistant of the scientist who had discovered the error.`  
分别改写成：a. 助手发现了错误；b. 科学家发现了错误。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q16" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.3.3 名词性从句概念包裹（第 17—24 题）

**不同内容示范**：I believe that the plan will work.（我相信计划会奏效。）中，that 从句作宾语；The belief that the plan will work gives us confidence.（计划会奏效的信念给我们信心。）中，从句说明 belief。第17—24题按指定作用造句，写完整主句。

> 对应理论：4.2.3节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q17"></div>


17. 把命题 `adequate sleep improves concentration` 放入主语位置，写成完整句。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q17" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q18"></div>


18. 把命题 `the new timetable will reduce congestion` 放入形式主语结构，使用 `It is likely that...`。

本题要求用指定句式表达“可能降低拥堵”的判断，允许把原句的确定语气改为可能性判断。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q18" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q19"></div>


19. 把命题 `the equipment failed because it had not been maintained` 放入宾语位置，主句使用 `The investigation revealed...`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q19" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q20"></div>


20. 把命题“最严重的问题是许多居民没有收到预警”放入表语位置。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q20" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q21"></div>


21. 把命题“学校可能延长图书馆开放时间”放入名词后的内容说明位置，锚点词使用 `possibility`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q21" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q22"></div>


22. 把直接疑问句 `Why did the river change course?` 改成：  
a. 主语从句；b. 宾语从句。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q22" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q23"></div>


23. 使用 `Whether...` 主语从句表达：“学校是否应该禁止课堂使用手机仍有争议。”


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q23" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

24. 改正并说明错误：  
`I wonder why did the organizers change the venue at the last minute.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q24" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.3.4 状语从句关系信号（第 25—32 题）

> 对应理论：4.2.4节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q25"></div>


25. 合并：`Several roads were flooded. The match was postponed.`  
要求：明确表达因果，但不要同时使用两个因果连接词。

用 because 或 so 连接同一对因果分句，不在同一层级重复写成 because ... so ...。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q25" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q26"></div>


26. 合并：`The initial results were encouraging. The researchers refused to make a firm claim.`  
要求：使用让步关系，分别写出前置版和后置版。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q26" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q27"></div>


27. 使用 `unless` 合并：`You must submit the form by Friday. Otherwise, your application will not be considered.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q27" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q28"></div>


28. 使用目的从句合并：`The speaker slowed down. She wanted the younger students to follow the explanation.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q28" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q29"></div>


29. 使用 `so...that...` 合并：`The wind was extremely strong. The ferry service had to be suspended.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q29" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q30"></div>


30. 使用时间关系合并：`The final bell rang. The students rushed out of the classroom.`  
要求：根据“铃响之后才冲出去”的顺序选择关系词。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q30" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q31"></div>


31. 使用 `while` 表示对比：`The new app is convenient for short messages. Email remains more suitable for formal communication.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q31" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q32"></div>


32. 改正并保留原逻辑：  
`Because the evidence was incomplete, therefore the committee delayed its decision.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q32" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

### 4.3.5 四大基础模型高阶进阶与压缩实战（第 33—44 题）

> 对应理论：4.2.1.1—4.2.1.4节、4.2.2.4—4.2.2.7节、4.2.3.3—4.2.3.5节、4.2.4.1—4.2.4.4节、4.3.2节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q33"></div>

33. **GPS 三槽位调度（主谓间双逗号插入位 + 双翼分流）**：  
将下列主干与三项背景/状态信息组装为一个节奏富于变化的单句，要求把“让步/困境信息”以双逗号插入主语和谓语之间，把“时间/背景”置于句首：  
主干：`The veteran captain steered the damaged vessel into the harbor.`  
信息：① 在暴风雪最猛烈的时刻（`at the height of the blizzard`）；② 尽管双目近乎被冰雨模糊且精疲力竭（`despite near-blinding sleet and extreme exhaustion`）；③ 凭借三十年的航海直觉（`by relying on thirty years of maritime instinct`）。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q33" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q34"></div>

34. **从句向介词短语“降维提速”压缩（Clause-to-PP Compression）**：  
在不改变原句核心逻辑的前提下，将下列三个主从复合句中的状语从句分别压缩为高密度的介词短语（分别使用 `Upon...`、`Prior to...`、`In the absence of...`）：  
a. `As soon as the lead investigator arrived at the crash site, the forensic team sealed off the perimeter.`  
b. `Before the nationwide carbon-trading scheme was launched, heavy industries faced few emission penalties.`  
c. `If there is no independent external auditing, corporate environmental pledges often remain empty rhetoric.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q34" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q35"></div>

35. **抽象空间名词定语从句（`where` vs. `which/that` 最小对立辨析与造句）**：  
判断下列 a、b 两句横线处应填入 `where` 还是 `which/that`，说明判定依据，并翻译 c 句（须使用 `stage / point + where`）：  
a. `The diplomatic talks have reached a delicate stage ______ any miscalculation could trigger a trade war.`  
b. `The diplomatic stage ______ the two ministers described yesterday requires careful handling.`  
c. 翻译：“现代大学应当营造一种包容的学术氛围，在这样的氛围中学生敢于质疑既有假设。”

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q35" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q36"></div>

36. **部分与整体量化定语从句（`Quantifier + of whom/which`）与逗号粘连纠错**：  
先将 a 组两句合并为含 `over 75% of whom` 的非限制性定语从句；再指出 b 句的语法红线并给出三种不同句法路径的修正方案：  
a. 合并：`The clinical trial enrolled 800 elderly patients. Over 75% of them showed significant cognitive improvement.`  
b. 纠错：`*The expedition discovered twelve ancient manuscripts, neither of them had been cataloged before.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q36" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q37"></div>

37. **定语从句“减重瘦身法则（Clause Reduction）”与不可删边界**：  
将下列能够减重的定语从句精简为分词短语、同位语或不定式短语；若某句**不能**直接删去 `which/who + be`，请说明原因：  
a. `Algorithms which are trained on biased historical data inevitably reproduce social inequalities.`  
b. `Dr. Morales, who is the chief architect of the satellite project, presented the telemetry report.`  
c. `She became the youngest mountaineer who successfully scaled K2 in winter.`  
d. `The safety regulations which might be revised next quarter have sparked heated debate.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q37" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q38"></div>

38. **`What` 聚焦型主语从句（伪裂句 Pseudo-Cleft）立论改写**：  
运用 `What + 从句 + is/was (not A, but) B` 构式改写下列平铺直叙的句子，将核心论点推向句末尾焦位置：  
a. 普通句：`The candidate's ability to remain calm under extreme pressure impressed the interviewers most.`  
b. 普通句：`True scientific literacy is defined not by the memorization of isolated facts, but by the capacity to evaluate evidence critically.`（提示：以 `What defines true scientific literacy...` 开头）

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q38" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q39"></div>

39. **形式宾语 `it` 构式（`Verb + it + Adj./N. + that / to do`）组装与纠错**：  
先改正 a 句的中式语序错误，再运用形式宾语 `it` 将 b 句译为地道英文：  
a. 纠错：`*The proliferation of smartphones has made that citizens access public services anytime convenient.`  
b. 翻译：“任何负责任的政府都不应把公众对公共卫生系统的信任视为理所当然（`take it for granted that...`）。”

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q39" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q40"></div>

40. **学术“壳名词（Shell Nouns）”同位语从句 vs. 定语从句终极辨析与造句**：  
判断 a、b 两句中 `assumption` 后的 `that` 从句分别是同位语从句还是定语从句，并用壳名词 `consensus`（共识）造一个包含同位语从句的完整学术句：  
a. `The assumption that economic growth alone will automatically resolve income inequality has been widely discredited.`  
b. `The assumption that the early economists made without empirical data proved flawed.`  
c. 造句：表达“气候学家之间已达成广泛共识：减少甲烷排放对于减缓近期全球变暖至关重要。”

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q40" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q41"></div>

41. **议论文黄金类比构式（`Just as A..., so (too) B...`）与条件构式（`provided that`）**：  
a. 运用 `Just as..., so (too)...` 将下列两组平行道理合成为一句富有哲理的议论文开篇句：  
   道理 A：`A sturdy bridge requires deep foundations to withstand turbulent currents.`  
   道理 B：`A resilient economy requires robust institutional safeguards to weather financial storms.`  
b. 运用 `provided that`（或 `on condition that`）合并：`Autonomous vehicles may operate on public highways. They must pass rigorous all-weather safety certifications.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q41" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q42"></div>

42. **状语从句的“分词化减重压缩（Adverbial Clause Reduction）”**：  
将下列三个状语从句减重压缩为“连词 + 分词/形容词短语”的高阶精炼构式：  
a. `Once the invasive species is introduced into an isolated island ecosystem, it can rapidly outcompete native wildlife.`  
b. `If this algorithmic vulnerability is left unaddressed, it could compromise millions of financial transactions.`  
c. `While the historians acknowledge the scarcity of written records, they have reconstructed the trade network through archaeological evidence.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q42" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q43"></div>

43. **状语从句减重压缩生死红线：垂悬修饰语（Dangling Modifier）诊断与修正**：  
指出下列句子为何在语法与认知逻辑上坍塌，并提供两种不同的修正方案（方案一：调整主句主语；方案二：补全从句主语）：  
原句：`*When viewed through an electron microscope, the lab technician noticed intricate crystalline patterns on the alloy surface.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q43" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q44"></div>

44. **介词短语依附歧义消解（PP Attachment Disambiguation）**：  
下列原句存在典型的多重挂靠歧义（`with a concealed weapon` 既可能被读作修饰动作 `detained` 的工具，也可能被读作修饰宾语 `the suspect` 的特征）：  
歧义原句：`The security officer detained the suspect with a concealed weapon in the corridor.`  
请分别改写为意思唯一、毫无歧义的两个句子：  
a. 表达“嫌疑人携带着暗藏的武器，保安在走廊里将其拘留”；  
b. 表达“在走廊里，保安使用暗藏的武器拘留了嫌疑人（或优先消除 `in the corridor` 与 `with a concealed weapon` 的多重挂靠歧义）”。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q44" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

## 4.4 名词化与事件关系表达（第 45—83 题）

### 4.4.1 介词修饰链与介词短语依附关系（第 45—52 题）

> 对应理论：3.4节、4.3.1节、4.3.2节、4.3.3节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q45"></div>


45. 将信息组装成一个名词短语：  
中心词：`decline`；对象：water quality；范围：three industrial regions；时间：the past decade。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q45" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q46"></div>


46. 将信息组装成一个名词短语：  
中心词：`debate`；主题：the use of facial recognition；场域：public transport；范围：several European cities。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q46" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q47"></div>


47. 将信息组装成一个名词短语：  
中心词：`shortage`；对象：trained nurses；机构范围：rural hospitals；地域：the western provinces。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q47" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q48"></div>


48. 将信息组装成一个名词短语：  
中心词：`change`；对象：students' attitudes；主题：online learning；时间：the first semester。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q48" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q49"></div>


49. 把两个并列关系压缩为一个对称分叉结构：  
`Prolonged drought affected crop yields. Rising fuel prices affected transport costs.`  
提示：以 `the effects` 为中心，使用 `of A on X and of B on Y`；本题写出名词短语即可，不另增加报告或研究行为。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q49" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q50"></div>


50. 把原因从句改写为“介词 + 逻辑主语 + 动名词”：  
`Because local residents opposed the plan, the council postponed the vote.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q50" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q51"></div>


51. 使用复合介词 `in the absence of` 改写：  
`Because there was no independent review, the figures could not be verified.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q51" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q52"></div>


52. 消除前置修饰语排列错误：  
`The recently opened near the railway station student mental health support center has extended its weekend hours.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q52" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.4.2 名词化与表示关系的动词（第 53—60 题）

> 对应理论：4.4.1节、4.4.2节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q53"></div>


53. 去掉 `because / so`，使用名词化和 `necessitate` 改写：  
`Because the virus mutated rapidly, researchers had to redesign the diagnostic test.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q53" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q54"></div>


54. 使用名词化和 `undermine` 改写：  
`Because the agency enforced the rule inconsistently, the public became less confident in the system.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q54" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q55"></div>


55. 使用名词化和 `obviate` 改写：  
`After the archive was digitized, researchers no longer needed to handle the fragile originals every day.`

题内设定：数字化使研究者每天接触原件不再必要；据此表达“消除需要”，不另添因果背景。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q55" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q56"></div>


56. 使用名词化和 `preclude` 改写：  
`Because the team failed to record the starting temperature, an accurate comparison was impossible.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q56" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q57"></div>


57. 减少空泛的 `there be`、`be` 和 `cause`：  
`There was a rapid growth in tourism, and this caused serious pressure on the island's water supply.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q57" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q58"></div>


58. 根据证据强度写一句完整英文：  
现有调查发现“长时间通勤”和“较低的工作满意度”有关，但没有证明直接因果。必须使用 `suggest`、`may`、`be associated with` 或同等强度表达。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q58" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q59"></div>


59. 为下列关系分别选择合适的表示关系的动词并写成完整句：  
a. 更低的安装成本促进家庭采用太阳能设备；  
b. 关键数据缺失阻止了独立验证；  
c. 持续的公众批评迫使委员会修改方案。  
可选：`encourage / facilitate / preclude / force / prompt / undermine`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q59" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q60"></div>


60. 把堆叠的动作名词改为清楚的动词表达，保留已给信息，不补出执行者；说明原文是否交代谁改进了流程：
`The implementation of the modernization of the registration process resulted in the achievement of a reduction in waiting time.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q60" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.4.3 非谓语动作链（第 61—68 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.1节、6.3节。

要求：在逻辑主语一致、动作顺序合理的前提下合并。不要为了使用非谓语而扭曲事件关系。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q61"></div>


61. Leo heard the fire alarm. He grabbed the emergency bag. He led the children toward the nearest exit.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q61" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q62"></div>


62. Priya reviewed the final chart. She noticed an unusual pattern. She called the research team immediately.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q62" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q63"></div>


63. The boy was shocked by the sudden applause. He stood still for a moment. He then bowed to the audience.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q63" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q64"></div>


64. The nurse carried a flashlight. She moved slowly through the dark corridor. She checked each room.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q64" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q65"></div>


65. The old bridge had been weakened by weeks of rain. It collapsed shortly after midnight. It blocked the only road into the village.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q65" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q66"></div>


66. The rain beat against the windows. The lights flickered. We continued packing the emergency supplies.  
要求：使用独立主格或 `with` 复合结构。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q66" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q67"></div>


67. 改正悬空修饰：  
`Walking into the laboratory, the broken container immediately caught Maya's attention.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q67" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q68"></div>


68. 合并并保留不同逻辑主语：  
`The sun was setting. The streetlights were coming on. We continued our search for the missing dog.`  
要求：使用独立主格或 `with` 复合结构。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q68" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.4.4 同位语与绝对结构（第 69—76 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.2节、4.5.3节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q69"></div>


69. 用总结性同位语替换 `which was a decision that...`：  
`The town converted an abandoned warehouse into a youth center, which was a decision that gave teenagers a safe place to meet.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q69" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q70"></div>


70. 用总结性同位语改写：  
`The hospital introduced a mobile appointment service, and this was an initiative that reduced travel for elderly patients.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q70" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q71"></div>


71. 使用恢复性同位语，承接前文并补充新说明：  
`The school made a firm commitment to inclusive education, ...`  
要求：重复 `commitment`，再说明这一承诺将如何落实。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q71" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q72"></div>


72. 用分项枚举型绝对结构改写：  
`The four students divided the work. One designed the poster, another contacted the speakers, a third booked the room, and the last managed online registration.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q72" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q73"></div>


73. 用 `with` 复合结构增加特写：  
`Ava waited outside the principal's office.`  
信息：双手紧握；目光盯着地板。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q73" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q74"></div>


74. 用原生独立主格增加特写：  
`The runner crossed the finish line.`  
信息：胸口剧烈起伏；双腿因疲惫而发抖。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q74" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q75"></div>


75. 用总结性同位语替换松散表达：  
`The company published all of its test results. This was a move that allowed independent researchers to check its claims.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q75" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q76"></div>


76. 把以下信息写成一个自然的右分支累积句：  
主干：`Nina opened the classroom door.`  
追加信息：教室里空无一人；窗户敞开；纸张散落在地板上；她意识到有人匆忙离开过。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q76" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

### 4.4.5 Halliday 7 大类关系动词事件连接实战（第 77—83 题）

> 对应理论：4.4节【句法的关系动词：语法隐喻与名词化压缩】。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q77"></div>

77. **反映/表现关系（Reflecting / Manifesting）**：将两个松散小句 `Consumers are purchasing more green products. This shows that public environmental awareness has reached an unprecedented level.`，运用名词化与反映关系动词（如 `reflect`, `manifest`, `mirror`, `indicate`）压缩装配为一个高阶学术复合句。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q77" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q78"></div>

78. **因果致使关系（Causal-Resultative）**：将口语化因果句 `Because the company rapidly automated its factories, many workers lost their jobs, and this caused severe social friction.`，压缩为由“原因名词化实体 + 因果关系动词（`trigger`, `precipitate`, `engender` 等）+ 结果名词化实体”驱动的高阶紧凑句。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q78" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q79"></div>

79. **构成/组成关系（Constitutive / Compositional）**：利用组成关系动词（如 `constitute`, `comprise`, `represent`, `form`），将“中小微企业在国家经济体系中占据核心支柱地位”表达为具有严密学术拓扑的句子，杜绝口语化的 `small businesses are very important`。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q79" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q80"></div>

80. **标志/界定关系（Signifying / Defining）**：将历史转折事件 `Scientists successfully edited human genes in 2012, and this began a whole new era in modern biotechnology.`，运用标志关系动词（`mark`, `signify`, `herald`, `characterize`）与名词化结构重塑为一个历史评注长句。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q80" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q81"></div>

81. **伴随/共生关系（Accompanying / Parallel）**：使用伴随与共生关系动词（`accompany`, `coincide with`, `parallel`），表达“经济的飞速繁荣往往伴随着生态环境的严峻承压”这一双变量复合事件。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q81" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q82"></div>

82. **促进/催化关系（Facilitative / Catalytic）**：运用促进关系动词（`facilitate`, `foster`, `catalyze`, `accelerate`），将“跨学科团队的频繁学术交流极大地促进了重大科学突破的诞生”表达为一个紧凑的高分句。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q82" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q83"></div>

83. **制约/决定关系（Governing / Conditioning）**：使用制约与决定关系动词（`dictate`, `govern`, `determine`, `shape`），将“一个社会的制度包容度深刻决定了其长期科技创新的活力与上限”转化为严谨学术句。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q83" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

## 4.5 信息焦点、高阶比较与准确性（第 84—107 题）

### 4.5.1 尾重、语态与倒装（第 84—91 题）

> 对应理论：4.6.1节、4.6.2节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q84"></div>


84. 使用形式主语 `It...that...`，避免头重脚轻：  
`That several villages had been excluded from the original survey was acknowledged by the research team.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q84" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q85"></div>


85. 改成更简洁的主动语态，并把长信息留在句末：  
`It was admitted by the committee that the new rule might disadvantage students without reliable internet access.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q85" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q86"></div>


86. 上文已经在讨论 `the samples`。把下面句子改为以已知信息开头的被动结构：  
`The technicians stored the samples at minus eighty degrees immediately after collection.`

本题中执行者不是关注点，允许省略 by the technicians；其余事件内容须保留。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q86" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q87"></div>


87. 用地点状语前置的完全倒装改写：  
`A small wooden cabin stood at the edge of the frozen lake.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q87" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q88"></div>


88. 用 `Only after...` 部分倒装改写：  
`We understood the scale of the damage only after the water had gone down.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q88" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q89"></div>


89. 用 `as / though` 让步倒装改写：  
`Although the task was difficult, the students refused to give up.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q89" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q90"></div>


90. 用 `Not until...` 部分倒装改写：  
`The town began to rebuild only when the last flood warning had been lifted.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q90" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q91"></div>


91. 调整信息顺序，使句末承载最长的新信息：  
`A plan that would provide free evening classes for adults who had left school early was proposed by the community center.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q91" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 4.5.2 高阶比较构式、倍数比例与平行对称实战（第 92—99 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.4节【高阶比较构式、倍数比例与平行对称铁律】。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q92"></div>

92. **比较项对称律与范畴错位修复**：诊断病句 `*The energy efficiency of electric vehicles is significantly higher than conventional gasoline cars.` 的范畴错位错误，并使用代词 `that of / those of` 写出两个语法合规且表达自然的改写句（分别针对单数指标与复数指标）。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q92" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q93"></div>

93. **句法结构范畴绝对平行（非谓语对非谓语）**：改正句子 `*Transitioning to renewable energy sources is more challenging than to decommission existing coal power plants.` 中的非对称错误，说明为什么比较构式要求前后两项句法层级绝对一致。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q93" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q94"></div>

94. **从句范畴绝对平行（名词性从句对从句）**：在句子 `What the preliminary laboratory tests demonstrated was far more remarkable than _______ (they had initially hypothesized).` 中，将括号内容补全为与前半句绝对平行的名词性从句，并解释其在学术写作中的论证力度。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q94" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q95"></div>

95. **准则评判构式 `more A than B`（与其说是 B 不如说是 A）**：将中文“此次供应链中断与其说是自然灾害所致，不如说是物流调度系统本身的脆弱性使然”翻译为地道的英文学术句，运用 `more A than B` 构式。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q95" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q96"></div>

96. **极度否定等同构式 `no more... than...`**：对比解释下列两句在认知逻辑与说话人确信度上的本质差异：
- a. `The proposed tax reform is not more effective than the previous policy.`
- b. `The proposed tax reform is no more effective than the previous policy.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q96" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q97"></div>

97. **双重比例构式与紧凑学术省略（`The more..., the more...`）**：将中文“宏观金融市场波动越剧烈，跨国企业的风险管理框架就越保守”翻译为地道英文，并写出一个省略系动词的紧凑学术版。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q97" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q98"></div>

98. **四大标准倍数句法槽位转换实战**：已知某新型量子芯片的运算速度是传统超级计算机的 5 倍。请分别使用以下三个槽位写出三个表达完全等义的英文学术句：
- a. 槽位一：`X is ... as [adj] as Y`
- b. 槽位二：`X is ... [comparative] than Y`
- c. 槽位三：`X is ... the speed of Y`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q98" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q99"></div>

99. **净增幅度（`increased by`）vs. 终态值（`increased to`）学术辨析**：分析句子 `Corporate investment in artificial intelligence research increased by 150% over the last fiscal year, reaching three times the projected budget.` 中 `increased by` 与 `increased to` 的逻辑差异，并指出为什么在学术汇报中绝不能混淆两者。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q99" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

每题先写出错误类型，再改正。

## 4.6 从好句子到好段落（第 108—120 题）

### 4.6.1 段落组织与衔接（第 108—115 题）

> 对应理论：4.7.2节、4.7.3节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q108"></div>


108. 将下列四句按“现象 → 影响 → 方案 → 结果”排序，并补出必要连接：  
A. `This shortage leaves many teenagers without a quiet place to study after school.`  
B. `Several neighborhoods have lost their public reading rooms in recent years.`  
C. `The city should therefore convert vacant shops into small community study centers.`  
D. `Such centers would provide both workspace and free internet access close to students' homes.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q108" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q109"></div>


109. 为下面一段选择最合适的主题句，并说明理由：  
后文信息：学校菜园让学生测量植物生长、记录天气、计算收成；科学与数学知识因此进入真实情境。  
A. `Our school has a garden behind the gym.`  
B. `School gardens can turn abstract classroom knowledge into practical experience.`  
C. `Many students enjoy spending time outdoors.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q109" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q110"></div>


110. 用 `This + 概括性名词` 把两句衔接起来：  
`The company replaced most in-person meetings with video calls.`  
`________ reduced travel costs but made informal communication more difficult.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q110" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q111"></div>


111. 改写下面三句，避免机械重复，并保持主题推进：  
`The school garden opened last year. The school garden is maintained by forty volunteers. The school garden now supplies vegetables to the cafeteria.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q111" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q112"></div>


112. 已知 Point 与 Evidence，请补写 Explanation 和 Link：  
Point: `Regular physical activity can improve students' ability to learn.`  
Evidence: `In one school survey, students who exercised before class reported higher concentration during the first two lessons.`  
要求：不能仅由这份相关调查断言已经证明因果，更不能把一个相关因素当成唯一原因。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q112" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q113"></div>


113. 根据证据强度改写段落，消除绝对化表达：  
`Social media destroys teenagers' mental health. A survey of 300 students proves that using social media causes anxiety in every user.`

题内证据：调查发现两者存在关联，没有证明因果。本题据此调整论断，不自拟额外研究数据。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q113" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q114"></div>


114. 根据中文信息写一个 4 句 PEEL 段落：  
主题：学校应提供更多急救培训。  
证据：上学期一名学生在体育课上受伤，受过培训的同学在老师到达前采取了正确措施。  
解释：基础知识能减少慌乱并争取时间。  
回扣：培训不能替代专业医疗，但能提高校园应急能力。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q114" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q115"></div>


115. 把下列流水账改成一个连贯段落，至少使用一个定语从句、一个非谓语结构和一个 `This + 概括性名词`：  
`Our class visited the recycling center. A guide welcomed us. She showed us the sorting machines. Some plastic was too dirty to recycle. We had not known this. We decided to reduce single-use packaging at school.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q115" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

### 4.6.2 四大主位推进模式段落拓扑实战（第 116—120 题）

> 对应理论：4.6节【语篇流动、焦点调度与词序调整】、4.7节【词汇层与语篇层】。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q116"></div>

116. **平行主位推进（Constant Theme Pattern）构建**：以 `Cognitive syntax`（认知句法学）为固定主位（$T_1$），分别从三个不同维度（$R_1$ 颠覆传统机械规则；$R_2$ 揭示人类体验基础；$R_3$ 搭建语言与大脑的桥梁）撰写一个 3 句式的平行主位连贯段落。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q116" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q117"></div>

117. **延续主位推进（Linear / Zig-zag Pattern）接力**：将上一句的新信息述位（$R_n$）提炼为下一句的主位（$T_{n+1}$），完成一个包含 3 步递进链条的语篇推进（链条：`工业化发展` → `化石能源过度消耗` → `温室气体浓度激增` → `全球极端气候危机`）。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q117" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q118"></div>

118. **派生主位推进（Derived Theme Pattern）总分展开**：首句提出一个宏观超主位（Hyper-Theme：`The revitalization of traditional culture depends on three crucial pillars.`），后接三句分别以派生子主位（$T_{1a}$ 青年群体的价值认同；$T_{1b}$ 现代数字技术的重构；$T_{1c}$ 产业化机制的持续赋能）展开论证。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q118" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q119"></div>

119. **交叉主位推进（Split Theme Pattern）精准分流**：首句述位提出两个并列复合命题（`The project faces dual challenges, encompassing both technological bottlenecks and institutional resistance.`），后接两句分别以 `The former...` 和 `The latter...` 为主位完成精准解构分流。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q119" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q120"></div>

120. **恶性主位跳跃诊断与修复**：诊断以下段落中的主位断裂跳跃问题，并运用线性延续主位链将其重组为行云流水的高阶段落：
`*Students often struggle with English writing. Teachers think grammar rules are essential. Long sentences are difficult to construct. Grammar exercises cannot guarantee fluency.`

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q120" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

## 4.7 定语从句写作与高级修订（第 121—132 题）

> 对应理论：4.2.2.3—4.2.2.8节。建议在第9—16题后补做；原21天安排保留，另用一次训练完成本节。

### 4.7.1 题目：从信息组装到修订

要求：保留给定事实，不补人物、因果或研究结论。允许的词形变化与句子拆分按各题说明执行。每题按“意义保留、结构正确、完成指令、理由准确”各1分核对，共48分；参考答案不是唯一措辞。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q121"></div>

**121. 两句合并：从句主语**

A girl found the key. The girl lives next door.
以“女孩找到了钥匙”为主句，用限定性定语从句说明是哪一个女孩；圈出两层谓语。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q121" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q122"></div>

**122. 两句合并：从句宾语**

The report is useful. Maya wrote the report yesterday.
以报告“有用”为主句合并；再写出省略关系词的版本，并说明为什么可以省略。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q122" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q123"></div>

**123. 地点名词不等于 where**

分别填入合适关系词，并还原 library 在从句中的作用：
A. This is the library ___ Maya designed.
B. This is the library ___ Maya works.
本题不改变 designed 和 works 后原本需要的关系。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q123" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q124"></div>

**124. 时间名词不等于 when**

分别填空并说明作用：
A. I remember the day ___ we first met.
B. I remember the day ___ we will never forget.
至少各给一种正确形式，不要求列出所有可能省略形式。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q124" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q125"></div>

**125. 所属关系与主谓一致**

The scientist received an award. The scientist's papers are widely read.
用 whose 合并，以“科学家获奖”为主句；说明 whose 后为什么用 are。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q125" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q126"></div>

**126. 介词来自原来的搭配**

Maya spoke to a teacher. The teacher helped her.
以“老师帮助了 Maya”为主句，分别写介词前置与介词留在从句末尾的版本。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q126" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q127"></div>

**127. 逗号与对象识别**

分别按语境合并 The tutor will give the talk. The tutor teaches chemistry.
A. 学校有几位导师，需要指出教化学的那位。
B. 双方已明确谈论同一位导师，任教学科只是补充信息。
比较两种写法的逗号，并说明 B 中能否用 that。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q127" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q128"></div>

**128. 纠错与关系词省略**

逐句改错；只修改必要部分，不改原来的动作主体：
A. The student who she wrote the report is absent.
B. The report which Maya wrote it is useful.
C. My report, Maya wrote yesterday, is useful.（报告已明确，从句为补充信息。）

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q128" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q129"></div>

**129. 两层主谓一致**

选出正确动词，并分别写明主句与从句的主语：
The reports that the student (write / writes) (is / are) useful.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q129" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q130"></div>

**130. which 指向名词还是事件**

分别说明 which 的指代，并把 B 改成两句明确表达原意：
A. The museum closed its east wing, which contained rare paintings.
B. The museum closed its east wing, which delayed the exhibition.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q130" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q131"></div>

**131. 非谓语压缩的意义边界**

判断下列改写是否在本题语境中完整保留原意，并说明理由：
A. the report which was published last week → the report published last week
B. the report which might be published next week → the report published next week
两处都指同一份报告；B 要明确保留“可能于下周发表”的判断，不把可能性改成确定安排。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q131" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q132"></div>

**132. 范围与歧义修订**

完成两项修订，不新增事实：
A. 表达“只有提交表格的学生才能参加出游”，并用定语从句限定 students。
B. The editor interviewed the scientist's assistant who had discovered the error. 已知发现错误的是助手，改写使人物关系清楚；允许分成两句。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q132" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

## 4.9 五步工程化造句与红线修正（第 143—182 题）

### 4.9.1 模块一：五步工程化工作流专项（第 143—162 题）

> [!cn-theory]
> **五步工程化工作流**（理论全书4.8.1节）：
> 1. **写清基本句子 Bone**：写出准确完整的核心命题，按事件与动词选择句型
> 2. **名词化压缩 Nominalize**：需要时将事件包装为名词，保留参与者和逻辑关系
> 3. **补充名词说明 Chain / Fork**：按搭配与依附补入必要限定，不固定层数
> 4. **右向延展 Branch**：按内容需要选择小句或修饰结构，不强制延展
> 5. **调整信息重点 Focus**：根据上下文安排焦点与尾重，必要时调整词序
>
> “全流程”要求检查五个方面，不要求每题同时使用五类结构。专项题明确指定的结构仍按题干完成；其他表达手段依内容选择。

#### 4.9.1.1 第 1 组：单步拆解（第 143—152 题，每题只做一步）

**SVO 示例**：Maya（主语 S）read（谓语动词 V）the report（宾语 O），意思是“Maya 读了报告”。本组先保留给定结构，再补题目所需说明；不要求所有英文句子都用 SVO。

> 对应理论：4.8.1节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q143"></div>


**143.（第一步·写清基本句子）** 把下面的意思写成最简的主语 + 谓语动词 + 宾语（SVO）结构。先写清谁做了什么，只删去不影响这层意思的细节。如果删掉名词后面的说明，就会说不清具体指谁或什么，那部分要保留。不规定词数：
> 央行大幅加息导致了房地产市场的急剧降温。

须保留“央行”这一主体来源，以及加息和市场放缓的程度信息。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q143" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q144"></div>


**144.（第一步·保留对象与范围）** 选择完整保留以下命题的句子，说明另两句遗漏或改变了什么，并标出所选句子的SVO主干：
> 这项新政策显著改善了低收入家庭的子女教育机会。
A. `The policy improved opportunities.`  
B. `The new policy significantly improved educational opportunities for children from low-income families.`  
C. `The new policy significantly improved educational opportunities for all children.`
<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q144" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q145"></div>


**145.（第二步·名词化压缩）** 将两个变化改写为名词化表达，保留因果和必须重设计的含义，改用单一主句组织信息，不再使用原来的 because / so 连接：
> Because the pathogen mutated rapidly, the vaccine's efficacy declined substantially, so researchers had to redesign the protocol.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q145" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q146"></div>


**146.（第二步·名词化压缩）** 将加息和市场降温名词化，保留 when 所表达的时间关系以及降温导致违约的结果；不额外断言加息导致降温：
> When the central bank raised interest rates abruptly, the housing market cooled rapidly, which caused a wave of mortgage defaults.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q146" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q147"></div>


**147.（第三步·补充名词说明）** 为中心名词 `decline` 构建 3 层介词修饰链（下降对象→地域范围→时间）：
> 提示：decline of ___ in ___ over ___


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q147" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q148"></div>


**148.（第三步·对称分叉）** 用对称分叉结构改写，确保 A-X、B-Y 对应：
> The impact of the policy on small businesses and the effect of inflation on consumers are both significant.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q148" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q149"></div>


**149.（第四步·右向延展）** 在以下主干后挂接一个限制性定语从句 + 一个表示结果的分词短语：
> The committee published the report.

允许自拟合理的报告内容与结果，作为假设写作场景；保留给定的报告事件。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q149" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q150"></div>


**150.（第四步·概念包裹）** 将以下命题包装为宾语从句，接在及物动词后：
> 命题：The algorithm may contain hidden biases.
> 动词：The audit revealed ___


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P4-Q150" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P4-Q151"></div>


**151.（第五步·总结性同位语）** 为以下句子添加一个总结性同位语（抽象名词 + that 从句），概括前面的事件，并在句末说明意义或结果；新增说明须与给定事实一致：
> The regulatory agency imposed strict caps on carbon emissions.

允许补充合理的假设背景，但须与给定事实一致，不把自拟内容当作真实史料。

### 4.9.2 模块二：句法十大红线诊断与修正（第 163—182 题）

> [!cn-theory]
> **十大红线**（理论全书4.8.2节）：
> 1. 前置修饰语排列错误（Left-Branching Trap）
> 2. 悬空修饰语（Dangling Modifiers）
> 3. 相隔较远的主谓单复数脱节（Long-Distance Disagreement）
> 4. 定语从句锚点歧义（Ambiguous Anchor Noun）
> 5. 关系信号灯多余叠加（Double-Signaling Fallacy）
> 6. 非限制性定语从句错用 that（The "That" in Comma Trap）
> 7. 疑问句打包语序未倒回（Wh- Inversion Error）
> 8. 同位语从句与定语从句功能混淆（That-Deletion Risk）
> 9. 滥用浅层系表结构（Nominal Depletion）
> 10. 句末说明是否有用（Anti-Climactic Ending）

#### 4.9.2.1 第 3 组：识别红线类型（第 163—172 题）

> 对应理论：4.8.2节。

> 每题先判断属于“语法错误”“依附歧义”还是“表达可优化”，再写检查项编号。十项中包含不同性质的问题，不能仅凭编号判错；尤其修饰对象不清可能是歧义，未必违反语法。
