### 5.3.4 名词化解压、动词关系与强度判断（第 16—20 题）

> 对应理论：5.2.3节、4.4.2节。

要求：把表示动作或变化的名词改为明确的动词表达，说明谁做了什么或什么发生变化，并判断加粗动词表示“促进、削弱、阻止、使必要、消除需要”中的哪一种关系。 未给出施事者时可用被动或说明变化，不自补研究者、公司等主体；表示“使必要”不等于已经实施。

### 5.3.5 段落阅读一：教育技术（第 21—24 题）

> 对应理论：5.5节、5.6节。

阅读短文并作答。

> [!cn-example]
> Although adaptive learning platforms can adjust the difficulty of exercises in response to student performance, their effectiveness depends heavily on the quality of the data used to guide those adjustments. In several schools, incomplete records caused the software to interpret temporary mistakes as lasting weaknesses, repeatedly assigning basic tasks to capable students. This pattern reduced opportunities for productive struggle, an outcome that may explain why some classes using the platform showed little improvement despite high completion rates.

### 5.3.7 段落阅读三：认知科学（第 29—32 题）

> 对应理论：5.5节、5.6节。

> [!cn-example]
> Participants studied a list of words either in moderate, unpredictable background noise or in silence. An immediate recall test showed little measurable difference between the groups. After a delay of forty-eight hours, however, those who had learned in noise remembered significantly fewer words than those who had learned in silence. The researchers therefore argue that distraction may interfere less with initial encoding than with the later stabilization of memory, a hypothesis that requires confirmation through direct measures of neural activity.

### 5.3.8 段落阅读四：算法治理（第 33—36 题）

> 对应理论：5.5节、5.6节。

> [!cn-example]
> What critics find most troubling about automated hiring systems is not simply that they may reproduce patterns found in historical data, but that the criteria by which candidates are rejected often remain inaccessible to those affected. Without a clear account of how particular features influence a decision, applicants cannot identify factual errors or challenge irrelevant assumptions. Transparency alone will not eliminate discrimination, but it is a necessary condition for meaningful review.

## 5.4 进阶解码专项：长句分层、消歧与高阶段落（第 37—82 题）

### 5.4.1 D0—D3 分层（第 37—41 题）

**不同题句示范**：After the meeting（会议后，D0）/ the students from Class Two（二班学生，D1）/ cleaned the room（打扫教室，D2）/ leaving it ready for the next class（让它可供下一节课使用，D3）。先圈主句，再恢复时间、对象范围和结果。按实际结构标注，不要求每句凑四部分。

> 对应理论：5.2节。

要求：用斜线划分句子，并标出以下成分。

- D0：前置背景、条件、让步或场景
- D1：完整主语，以及说明它是哪一个／哪一类的成分
- D2：谓语及所需宾语、补足内容，如 force someone to act 中的 someone 和 to act
- D3：另加的事实、状态、结果或评价；是否算 D3 需结合上下文判断，位于句末的成分不一定都是 D3

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q37"></div>


37. Although remote work has broadened access to urban jobs, the uneven availability of reliable internet in rural communities has prevented some employees from taking full advantage of these opportunities, reinforcing existing regional inequalities.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q37" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q38"></div>


38. Following several weeks of overcrowding, the decision by regional hospitals to share spare-bed data through a single platform enabled emergency coordinators to redirect patients more efficiently.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q38" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q39"></div>


39. Because estimates of long-term maintenance costs remain uncertain, the recommendation that the city delay construction until additional soil tests are completed has gained support among local residents.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q39" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q40"></div>


40. What began as a small volunteer project to digitize family records has evolved into a national archive, giving researchers access to documents that might otherwise have been lost.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q40" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q41"></div>

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q41" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 5.4.2 中心词、谓语与主谓一致（第 42—46 题）

> 对应理论：5.2.2节、4.8.2.3节。

要求：在题句上框出完整主语；名词短语主语另圈中心词。选择正确谓语，写一个可能干扰判断的内部名词。若整个从句作主语，按整体意义判断数，不机械寻找链尾名词。

### 5.4.4 名词化解压、动词关系与强度判断（第 52—56 题）

> 对应理论：5.2.3节、4.4.2节。

要求：把表示动作或变化的名词改为明确的动词表达，说明谁做了什么或什么发生变化，并判断加粗动词表示“诱发或催化、驱动或促进、削弱或瓦解、阻断或排除、迫使或要求、凸显或印证”中的哪一类关系（分类表见理论全书 4.4.2.1 节）。 未给出施事者时可用被动或说明变化，不自补研究者、公司等主体；表示“使必要”不等于已经实施。

> Although text-message reminders increased attendance at medical appointments during the first month of the trial, the effect was much smaller among patients whose work schedules changed from week to week. The researchers therefore caution that reminders can reduce missed visits caused by forgetfulness, but cannot by themselves remove practical barriers to rescheduling.

### 5.4.7 段落阅读三：信息与记忆（第 65—68 题）

> 对应理论：5.5节、5.6节。

> [!cn-example]
> When volunteers read corrections immediately after seeing misleading headlines, most could identify the false claims in a test conducted the same day. One week later, however, those who had encountered the headlines repeatedly were more likely to accept the claims than those who had seen them only once, even though both groups had read the same corrections. The researchers suggest that repetition may create a sense of familiarity that outlasts memory for the correction, an explanation that still needs to be tested under conditions in which exposure frequency is carefully controlled.

> 对应理论：5.5节、5.6节。

> [!cn-example]
> What clinicians object to in the new triage system is not that an algorithm participates in the decision, but that uncertain cases can be assigned a low priority without any explanation of the evidence used. Unless the interface shows which observations shaped the recommendation, medical staff cannot correct missing data or judge whether an unusual symptom was ignored. Explanations will not make every automated recommendation reliable, but they are necessary for responsible clinical review.

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q78"></div>

78. **早期闭合与及物性陷阱（Early Closure Trap）断句**：句子 `While the committee was investigating the allegations of fraud emerged in the national press.` 容易让读者产生怎样的假性解读？请标出引发句法崩溃的“碰撞词”，并使用逗号和结构括号写出消歧改写。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q78" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q79"></div>

79. **缩略关系从句与被动分词误接（Reduced Relative Clause Trap）**：分析长难句 `The synthetic molecules synthesized by the automated biochemical laboratory failed to bind to target receptors under ambient temperature.` 中，为什么读者初次阅读时容易把 `synthesized` 误读为谓语动作？请写出还原隐性关系词后的显性从句版。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q79" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q80"></div>

80. **引导词 that 省略引发的论元归属断裂（Complementizer Drop Trap）**：在学术长句 `The structural engineer recognized the critical foundation flaw could lead to catastrophic collapse within days.` 中，标出发生论元角色跃迁的名词短语，说明为什么正式学术写作强烈建议保留连接词 `that`。

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q80" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q81"></div>

81. **双及物与名词短语边界歧义（Double Object vs. Embedded Subject）**：分析句子 `The research director promised the junior scholars who arrived late would receive a secondary briefing.` 中，为什么大脑会把 `the junior scholars` 误读为 `promised` 的间接宾语？请给出两步实时修订过程。

82. **依存局部性理论（DLT）工作记忆负荷量化与改写**：对比下列两句在工作记忆中的“存储成本”与“整合成本”，解释为什么版本 B 比版本 A 更加加工友好，并将其译为中文：
- A: `The report that the analyst whom the committee appointed yesterday drafted was approved.`
- B: `The committee yesterday appointed an analyst, who drafted a report that was approved.`

## 5.5 读写转化与长难句综合改写（第 83—106 题）

### 5.5.1 GPS 场景定位（第 83—88 题）

**SVO 示例**：Maya（主语 S）read（谓语动词 V）the report（宾语 O），意思是“Maya 读了报告”。本组先保留给定结构，再补题目所需说明；不要求所有英文句子都用 SVO。

> 对应理论：4.2.1节。

要求：保留给定句子的主语、谓语及必要补足内容，把其余信息放入合适位置。句型按实际结构判断：wait for 的 for 短语不是直接宾语。不要把所有修饰语堆到名词前。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q83"></div>


83. 主干：`The school will hold a science exhibition.`  
信息：下周五下午；在新图书馆一楼；为了展示学生的创新项目。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q83" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q84"></div>


84. 主干：`The volunteers distributed food packages.`  
信息：暴雨过后的第二天清晨；在河边的临时避难所；在当地医生的协助下。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q84" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q85"></div>


85. 主干：`The researchers released the report.`  
信息：经过三年的实地调查；在一次国际会议上；为了推动对湿地的保护。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q85" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q86"></div>


86. 主干：`The boy waited for the rescue team.`  
信息：在漆黑的山洞里；双手紧紧抱住膝盖；整个夜晚。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q86" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q87"></div>


87. 主干：`The committee revised the policy.`  
信息：面对公众持续批评；在周一的紧急会议上；为保护临时工的权益。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q87" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q88"></div>


88. 主干：`The old woman opened the letter.`  
信息：在壁炉旁；黄昏时；手指因激动而微微颤抖。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q88" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 5.5.2 定语从句与信息嫁接（第 89—94 题）

> 对应理论：4.2.2节。

要求：合并句子，并根据语义选择限定性或非限制性定语从句。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q89"></div>


89. The students completed the field survey. The students will present their findings tomorrow.  
限定范围：只有完成调查的学生参加展示。

须保留“只有”的排他含义；仅使用限定性定语从句不能自动表达这一含义。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q89" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q90"></div>


90. Professor Lin will deliver the opening speech. Professor Lin has studied rural education for twenty years.  
人物身份已明确，后句为补充信息。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q90" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q91"></div>


91. The city restored the old theater. The decision attracted new visitors to the district.  
要求：用修饰整个前句的 `which`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q91" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q92"></div>


92. We visited a village. Many traditional wooden houses have survived in the village.  
要求：使用 `where`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q92" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q93"></div>


93. The laboratory developed a low-cost sensor. The sensor can detect unsafe levels of lead in drinking water.  
要求：避免重复 `sensor`。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q93" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q94"></div>


94. The museum has launched an online archive. Through the archive, users can examine rare manuscripts.  
要求：使用“介词 + 关系代词”。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q94" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 5.5.3 概念包裹与关系信号灯（第 95—100 题）

**先看任务形式**：I know that Maya is at home. 中，that 从句作宾语；The news that Maya is at home reassures Leo. 中，从句说明 news 的内容。按本题新内容造句，不能只写孤立从句。

> 对应理论：4.2.3节、4.2.4节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q95"></div>


95. 把“daily exercise improves concentration”这段意思分别作主语、宾语、表语，或在名词后说明内容，写四个完整句子；圈出从句并标作用。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q95" data-lines="8" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q96"></div>


96. 把直接疑问句 `Why did the experiment fail?` 改成：  
a. 主语从句；b. 宾语从句。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q96" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q97"></div>


97. 合并：The evidence remains incomplete. The committee approved a limited pilot program.  
要求：使用让步关系，写出前置版和后置版。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q97" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q98"></div>


98. 合并：The river level continued to rise. The emergency team evacuated the village.  
要求：用明确的因果信号，不夸大因果强度。

题内设定：河水持续上涨是撤离的原因之一。表达这一因果关系，不写成唯一原因。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q98" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q99"></div>


99. 合并：The new timetable saves commuting time. Some students still prefer the old schedule.  
要求：使用 `while` 表示对比，不写成时间关系。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q99" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q100"></div>


100. 改写：`Because the sample was too small, so the researchers avoided a firm conclusion.`  
要求：消除双重信号，并保留“样本过小”这一限制。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q100" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


### 5.5.4 介词修饰链、名词化与表示关系的动词（第 101—106 题）

> 对应理论：3.4节、4.3节、4.4节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q101"></div>


101. 将信息组装成一个名词短语：  
中心词：`decline`；对象：manufacturing output；范围：three coastal provinces；时间：the past five years。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q101" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q102"></div>


102. 将信息组装成一个名词短语：  
中心词：`debate`；主题：the ethical use of facial recognition；场域：public schools；范围：several European countries。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q102" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q103"></div>


103. 把两个过程名词化并用表示关系的动词连接：  
“城市迅速扩张，因此湿地面积大幅减少。”

保留原句的因果强度与程度，不额外添加“突然发生”或“加速”的含义。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q103" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q104"></div>


104. 把两个过程名词化并用表示关系的动词连接：  
“研究人员没有共享原始数据，这使独立验证无法进行。”


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q104" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q105"></div>


105. 改写，减少空泛的 `be` 与 `cause`:  
`There was a rapid increase in fuel prices, and this caused serious problems for small transport companies.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q105" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q106"></div>


106. 根据证据强度选择合适表达，并写成完整句：  
现有研究显示“社交媒体使用”和“青少年睡眠不足”有关，但尚未证明直接因果。必须使用 `may`、`be associated with` 或同等强度表达。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q106" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


---

## 5.6 高阶右分支与综合改写（第 107—135 题）

### 5.6.1 综合改写第一组（第 107—118 题） 

#### 5.6.1.1 非谓语动作链（第 107—110 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.1节。

要求：在不改变事件先后和逻辑主语的前提下，将短句改成自然的动作链。不要为了“高级”而强行合并。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q107"></div>


107. Maya heard a child calling for help. She dropped her bag. She ran toward the river. She searched the dark water.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q107" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q108"></div>


108. The speaker looked across the crowded hall. He took a slow breath. He unfolded his notes. He began the presentation calmly.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q108" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q109"></div>


109. The dog had been trapped under the fallen fence. It struggled to stand. It followed the firefighter toward safety.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q109" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q110"></div>


110. The rain struck the windows. The lights flickered above us. We waited in silence for the power to return.  
要求：至少使用一个独立主格或 `with` 复合结构。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q110" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


#### 5.6.1.2 同位语与绝对结构（第 111—114 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.2节、4.5.3节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q111"></div>


111. 用总结性同位语改写：  
`The town converted an abandoned factory into a public library, which was a decision that gave young people a safe place to study.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q111" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q112"></div>


112. 用总结性同位语改写：  
`The medical team created a mobile clinic for remote villages, and this was an initiative that greatly improved access to basic care.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q112" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q113"></div>


113. 用分项枚举型绝对结构改写：  
`The four teams examined different aspects of the problem. One analyzed costs, another studied public opinion, a third tested technical feasibility, and the last reviewed legal risks.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q113" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q114"></div>


114. 用原生独立主格或 `with` 复合结构增加一个“特写镜头”：  
`Daniel stepped onto the stage and faced the audience.`  
可用信息：心跳很快；双手冰凉；聚光灯照在脸上。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q114" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


#### 5.6.1.3 信息焦点与倒装（第 115—118 题）

> 对应理论：4.6节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q115"></div>


115. 用地点状语前置的完全倒装改写：  
`A narrow stone bridge stood at the end of the forest path.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q115" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q116"></div>


116. 用 `Only after...` 部分倒装改写：  
`She understood the value of patience only after the project failed twice.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q116" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q117"></div>


117. 用 `as / though` 让步倒装改写：  
`Although the climb was exhausting, the children refused to turn back.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q117" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q118"></div>


118. 调整信息顺序，使最长、最新的信息位于句末：  
`That the treatment could produce serious side effects in patients with weakened immune systems was acknowledged by the researchers.`  
要求：使用形式主语或主动语态，任选一种。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q118" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


---

### 5.6.2 综合改写第二组（第 119—130 题） 

#### 5.6.2.1 非谓语动作链（第 119—122 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.1节。

要求：在不改变事件先后和逻辑主语的前提下，将短句改成自然的动作链。不要为了“高级”而强行合并。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q119"></div>


119. Elena smelled smoke in the hallway. She alerted her neighbors. She turned off the gas supply. She guided two children outside.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q119" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q120"></div>


120. The cyclist noticed a fallen branch ahead. He slowed down. He signaled to the riders behind him. He moved carefully around the obstruction.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q120" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q121"></div>


121. The footbridge had been damaged by the storm. It was closed for repairs. Residents took a two-kilometer detour to reach the market.


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q121" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q122"></div>


122. The wind pushed against the sails. Waves broke over the deck. The crew continued to secure the damaged mast.  
要求：至少使用一个独立主格或 `with` 复合结构。


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q122" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


#### 5.6.2.2 同位语与绝对结构（第 123—126 题）

> 对应理论：4.5.2节、4.5.3节。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q123"></div>


123. 用总结性同位语改写：  
`The council turned an empty parking lot into a public garden, which was a decision that created a cooler gathering place for nearby residents.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q123" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q124"></div>


124. 用总结性同位语改写：  
`The school introduced free breakfast for all pupils, and this was an initiative that improved morning attendance.`


<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q124" data-lines="4" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>


<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q125"></div>


125. 用分项枚举型绝对结构改写：

### 5.6.3 篇章级阅读图谱与宏观论证树图破译（第 131—135 题）

> 对应理论：5.6节【篇章级阅读：从句子进阶到篇章图谱】。

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q131"></div>

131. **两级论证树图（Argument Tree）抽取**：精读以下学术文本，抽取出该段落的**核心主张（Core Claim）** 与支撑该主张的两个**并列支链论据（Sub-arguments A & B）**，并指出连接支链的逻辑路标词：
> While conventional educational dogma asserts that artificial intelligence tools necessarily induce cognitive atrophy among language learners, empirical findings demonstrate that interactive AI systems actually alleviate extraneous working-memory load. On the one hand, real-time syntactic parsing assists novice writers in monitoring modifier attachment ambiguities without exhausting attentional resources. On the other hand, adaptive automated feedback prompts metacognitive reflection by requiring students to actively justify their stylistic revisions against corpus evidence.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q131" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q132"></div>

132. **让步与反驳树状结构破译**：阅读包含多重曲折转折的学术论述，梳理出其“让步（Concession） → 反驳（Rebuttal） → 修正立场（Refined Thesis）”的三阶认知图谱，并指出作者最终支持的命题：
> Admittedly, quantitative performance metrics in automated grading software boast unparalleled scalability and cost-efficiency. Skeptics rightly note that these algorithms can evaluate thousands of essays in seconds. Nevertheless, such surface-level indices remain fundamentally blind to ideological nuance, emotional sincerity, and culturally grounded metaphor. Consequently, reliance solely on algorithmic scores risks penalizing non-standard yet genuinely creative prose, necessitating a hybrid pedagogical model that preserves irreplaceable human evaluation.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q132" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q133"></div>

133. **级联因果链图解抽取**：精读以下生态学语篇，提取由 3 个节点构成的**因果级联链条（Causal Cascade Chain）**（事件A → 导致机制B → 引发最终生态后果C）：
> Elevated sea-surface temperatures trigger widespread thermal stress among symbiotic algae, precipitating catastrophic coral bleaching across tropical barrier reefs. The subsequent mortality of these structural reef ecosystems inevitably deprives thousands of specialized marine organisms of their foundational feeding and nursery habitats, ultimately resulting in an alarming collapse of coastal biodiversity.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q133" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q134"></div>

134. **宏观超主位（Hyper-Theme）与段落推进图解**：阅读以下包含两个段落的微型篇章，分别写出段落一与段落二的宏观超主位（Hyper-Theme），并绘制两段之间如何通过“问题诊断 → 解决方案”建立宏观篇章衔接：
> Paragraph 1: Traditional urban traffic planning has reached a catastrophic impasse due to its relentless reliance on car-centric infrastructure. Expanding highway lanes consistently fails to alleviate gridlock, instead inducing latent vehicular demand and generating immense carbon emissions that degrade public health.
> Paragraph 2: A transformative resolution to this systemic crisis lies in the rapid deployment of transit-oriented multimodal mobility. By synchronizing high-speed commuter rail, dedicated cycling corridors, and pedestrianized public squares, progressive municipalities can revitalize urban density while slashing vehicular emissions.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q134" data-lines="5" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

<div class="question-start" data-question="P5-Q135"></div>

135. **阅读解码逆向转化为 Toulmin 图尔敏论证模型**：精读论证段落，将其逆向解码为 Toulmin 论证骨架：**主张（Claim）**、**数据事实（Data/Grounds）**、**正当理由（Warrant）**、**反驳考量（Rebuttal）**：
> Extensive reading of authentic literature unequivocally accelerates second-language syntactic acquisition. Longitudinal tracking of high school learners reveals that those engaged in sustained fiction reading produced 40% more multi-clause right-branching sentences than peers restricted to textbooks. This dramatic advantage stems from the neural entrainment of complex syntactic patterns through rich, emotionally engaged immersion. Although rote drill proponents argue that explicit rule instruction yields faster initial test gains, such mechanical knowledge fails to sustain spontaneous production under real-time communication pressure.

<div class="answer-space" data-question="P5-Q135" data-lines="6" aria-label="作答区域"><div class="answer-label">作答</div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div><div class="writing-line"></div></div>

## 5.8 建议练习安排与训练记录

- 第 1—2 天：第 1—20 题（基础句法分层、中心词与名词化解压）
- 第 3—4 天：第 21—36 题（基础学术段落阅读四篇）
- 第 5—6 天：第 37—56 题（进阶长难句分层、主谓一致与关系动词强度）
- 第 7—8 天：第 57—82 题（高阶学术段落四篇与花园路径结构消歧）
- 第 9—10 天：第 83—106 题（读写转化：四大基础模型综合改写）
- 第 11—12 天：第 107—135 题（高阶右分支综合改写与篇章宏观论证树图破译）
- 第 13—14 天：第 136—151 题（句法红线实战诊断），并重做全篇错题

完成第一轮后，将答案遮住再做第二轮。第二轮重点不是追求更复杂的句子，而是减少回读、保持主干清楚，并准确保留限定范围和证据强度。

---

## 5.9 参考答案与逆向解码图解

### 5.9.0 阅读核心模型训练参考答案与精解（9 组）

> [!cn-warning]
> 先独立完成 5.2 节 9 组核心模型训练，再对照本节答案核对切分边界、中心词锁定与证据强度。

- **阅读组 1 · D0 句首背景切割**：
  - **D0 边界**：`In the event of relativistic plasma jets failing to penetrate the dense interstellar medium,`（由复合介词 `In the event of` 引导的“介词 + 逻辑主语 `relativistic plasma jets` + 动名词短语 `failing to...`”条件背景层）；
  - **主句起点**：主句从 **`the dissipation`**（`the dissipation of kinetic energy precludes star formation`）正式开始。
  - *解码解析*：遇到句首长介词短语或状语从句时，第一步先顺读至主句前的逗号，将整个 D0 标为“条件/背景前提”，随后迅速抓住主句核心主谓宾 `dissipation precludes star formation`（动能耗散阻止了恒星形成）。
- **阅读组 2 · 穿透介词链锁定主语中心词与谓语**：
  - **中心名词**：复数名词 **`results`**；
  - **对应谓语**：复数被动谓语 **`were published`**；
  - **易误判就近名词**：主语内部挂载了 4 层介词短语（`of the latest study` → `on the effects` → `of climate change` → `on the migration patterns` → `of birds` → `in the northern hemisphere`），读者若不穿透介词链，极易把紧挨谓语的单数名词 `hemisphere` 或局部复数名词 `birds` 误判为主语。
- **阅读组 3 · 区分完整主语组块与中心词**：
  - **完整主语**：`The unprecedented acceleration of the contraction of liquidity in interbank markets across major advanced economies`（从句首冠词 `The` 一直延伸至谓语 `precipitated` 之前）；
  - **主语中心词**：单数抽象名词 **`acceleration`**（谓语动词为 **`precipitated`**）；
  - **内部干扰名词**：`contraction`、`liquidity`、`markets`、`economies` 均为后置介词链内部的修饰成分，共同说明“什么领域、什么指标的加速”，不能替代中心词 `acceleration`。
- **阅读组 4 · 高频学术关系动词语义矩阵与边界**：
  - `precipitate`（**突发促成 / 急剧引发**）：`The sudden disclosure of accounting fraud precipitated a massive sell-off.`（会计欺诈的突然曝光 → 急剧引发大规模抛售；强调结果发生的突然性与紧迫性）；
  - `undermine`（**逐渐削弱 / 暗中动摇**）：`Repeated data fabrication undermined public confidence in clinical trials.`（反复的数据造假 → 削弱公众对临床试验的信任）；
  - `preclude`（**彻底阻断 / 排除……的可能性**）：`The absence of baseline measurements precluded a reliable comparison.`（缺乏基线测量数据 $\nrightarrow$ 使可靠比较成为不可能）；
  - `necessitate`（**使……成为必要 / 客观要求**）：`Structural cracks in the dam necessitated an immediate engineering overhaul.`（大坝出现结构性裂缝 → 使立即开展工程大修成为必要；**注意**：`necessitate` 仅断言“客观上必须做”，本身并不保证大修已经完成）；
  - `obviate`（**消除……的必要性 / 免除**）：`Automated cloud backup obviated the need for daily manual tape archiving.`（自动云端备份 → 消除了每日人工磁带归档的需要；**注意**：`obviate the need for X` 意为“不再需要 X”，绝不等于“明令禁止 X”）。
- **阅读组 5 · 提取宾语从句核心发现并严守证据边界**：
  - **核心发现**：分配权重的注意力机制可能会被微小的噪声干扰而失去稳定（`the attention mechanisms which allocate weights could be destabilized by minor noise`）；
  - **证据边界**：该结论源自“一项定量分析（`A quantitative analysis demonstrated that...`）”，且从句内部使用了情态动词 **`could`**，表明这是一种**潜在脆弱性与可能性风险**，阅读选项若将其偷换为“微小噪声必然摧毁所有注意力机制”则属于过度推断。
- **阅读组 6 · 句末右分支补充与作者态度评价识别**：
  - **态度评价词**：**`severely compromised`**（严重损害了国际信任，带有显著负面评价色彩）；
  - **所在句法载体**：句末挂载的**总结性同位语（Summative Appositive）** `an outcome that severely compromised international trust`（前接现在分词结果状语 `effectively shifting the burden onto vulnerable regions`）。
  - *解码解析*：英文学术长句常在主句陈述客观事实（条约在无强制赔偿条款下获批），而在句末 D3 层（分词短语 + 总结性同位语）注入真正的后果定性与作者批判立场。
- **阅读组 7 · 非限制性关系代词 `which` 的事件指代还原**：
  - **指代判定**：句末 `, which constituted a violation of international standards` 中的 `which` 指代的是前面**整个主句事件**（“该财团未能披露安全数据 `The consortium failed to disclose the safety data`”这一失职行为），而**绝不是**指代紧挨逗号的名词 `the safety data`（安全数据本身并不构成违规，“隐瞒不报”才构成违规）。
- **阅读组 8 · 外刊篇章段落功能标签与主旨预判**：
  - **核对标准**：为所选文章的每一段准确标注功能语步标签（如 `Move 1: 传统共识/现象引入` → `Move 2: 实证反常/研究缺口` → `Move 3: 新机制阐释與证据支撑` → `Move 4: 现实政策启示与边界限定`），并在看题干选项前独立写出包含“核心议题 + 作者核心论断 + 证据强度”的一句话主旨。
- **阅读组 9 · 语篇回指链（`This / These / Such + 外壳名词`）追踪**：
  - **核对标准**：圈出文中所有 `This pattern / These findings / Such an approach` 等回指节点，逐一用箭头连回前一句或前一段被打包的具体事件小句。只要有一处指不出具体前文内容，即定位为阅读理解的“断链点”，需立即回溯前句谓语与宾语。

---

### 5.9.1 基础解码专项参考答案（第 1—36 题）

> [!cn-theory]
> 先独立完成，再核对。开放题的答案只是示范；只要结构合法、语义准确、信息层级清楚，就可以成立。

#### 5.9.1.1 第 1—5 题

1. D0 `Despite initial resistance from local residents` / D1 `the introduction of a congestion charge in the city center` / D2 `has reduced peak-hour traffic` / D3 `encouraging officials to expand the program`。  
   *解析*：D0 为 `Despite` 引导的让步背景介词短语；D1 主语中心词为名词化动作 `introduction`；D2 核心谓语与宾语陈述“减少了高峰期车流”；D3 用现在分词短语作自然结果状语，引出后续政策扩展。

2. D0 `Under conditions of prolonged drought` / D1 `the decline in groundwater levels across the northern plain` / D2 `has forced many farmers to abandon water-intensive crops` / 无独立 D3。  
   *解析*：D0 用介词短语交代持续干旱的环境前提；D1 主语中心词为 `decline`（后接两层介词短语限定对象与地域）；D2 包含核心谓语 `has forced` 及其复合宾语补足结构 `many farmers to abandon...`，句末无独立悬挂的 D3 分词或同位语层。

3. D0 `Although the preliminary findings appeared promising` / D1 `the absence of long-term evidence from large clinical trials` / D2 `prevented regulators from approving the treatment` / 无独立 D3。  
   *解析*：D0 为 `Although` 引导的让步状语从句（先承认初步结果向好）；D1 主语中心词为否定性抽象名词 `absence`；D2 谓语结构 `prevented... from approving...` 直接点明监管未予批准的核心结果。

4. D0 `In response to repeated disruptions in the global supply chain` / D1 `the decision by several manufacturers to relocate key facilities closer to consumer markets` / D2 `has become increasingly common` / D3 `a shift likely to reshape regional employment`。  
   *解析*：D0 用复合介词 `In response to` 交代供应链中断的触发原因；D1 主语中心词为 `decision`（后接施事 `by...` 与内容不定式 `to relocate...`）；D2 中 `increasingly common` 是系动词 `has become` 后的表语，属于主干核心不可拆入 D3；真正的 D3 是句末总结性同位语 `a shift likely to reshape regional employment`。

5. D1 是整个 `What...behavior` 主语从句；D2 为 `has become a lasting transformation`（`in retail` 补充范围）；D3 为 `affecting both store design and delivery networks`。本句没有独立 D0。  
   *解析*：本句无前置背景状语（无 D0），直接以 `What` 引导的名词性主语从句充当 D1 逻辑起点；D2 完成“演变为持久转型”的系表论断；D3 用现在分词短语 `affecting...` 补充说明该转型波及的具体方面。

#### 5.9.1.2 第 6—10 题（中心词、谓语与主谓一致）

6. **完整主语**：`The effects of repeated sleep loss on memory consolidation in adolescents`；**中心词**：复数名词 **`effects`** → 选复数系动词 **`are`**。  
   *解析*：主语后挂载了三层介词短语（`of repeated sleep loss`、`on memory consolidation`、`in adolescents`）；其中不可数单数名词 `loss` 和 `consolidation` 极易构成就近干扰，但主谓一致必须穿透介词链锁定句首复数中心词 `effects`。

7. **完整主语**：`A series of unexpected failures in the cooling systems of the research facility`；**中心词**：单数集合名词 **`series`** → 选单数助动词 **`has`**。  
   *解析*：`A series of + 复数名词`（一系列……）的语法中心词是单数名词 `series`（强调“一整套连续事件”），因此谓语动词严格用单数 `has`，切勿被后面的复数名词 `failures` 或 `systems` 误导。

8. **完整主语**：`The quality of the arguments presented by the two witnesses during the final hearing`；**中心词**：单数不可数名词 **`quality`** → 选第三人称单数谓语 **`depends`**。  
   *解析*：`of the arguments` 及过去分词短语 `presented by the two witnesses...` 均是修饰 `quality` 的后置定语；决定谓语单复数的是“质量（`quality`）”而非复数干扰词 `arguments` 或 `witnesses`。

9. **完整主语**：`What the committee members described as minor administrative delays`（`What` 引导的名词性主语从句）→ 选单数系动词 **`was`**。  
   *解析*：整个 `What` 从句打包指代“被委员会成员描述为轻微行政延误的那一现象”，并与单数表语 `a sign of deeper institutional weakness` 呼应，整体视为单数概念；从句内部的复数名词 `members` 和 `delays` 处于从句层级，不能越级控制主句系动词。

10. **完整主语**：`The data collected from more than thirty monitoring stations along the coast`；**中心词**：**`data`** → 按研究文体复数用法选 **`indicate`**。  
    *解析*：`collected from more than thirty monitoring stations along the coast` 为过去分词短语作后置定语。在严谨学术与科技文体中，`data` 作为 `datum` 的复数形式常配复数动词 `indicate`（在现代通用英语中也可作不可数名词配单数谓语）。

#### 5.9.1.3 第 11—15 题（从句边界、锚点与指代）

11. `which` 指代先行词名词短语 **`the sample`**（那个样本）。  
    *解析*：非限制性定语从句谓语 `had been stored at room temperature for six hours`（在室温下存放了六个小时）描述的是物理实体“样本（`the sample`）”自身的存放经历。

12. `which` 指代前面**整个主句事件**——即“**实验室拒收该样本**（`The laboratory rejected the sample`）**这一整件事**”。  
    *与第 11 题的本质区别*：第 11 题的 `which` 锚定具体名词 `the sample`；而第 12 题中，导致整个试验延期（`delayed the entire trial`）的原因并不是样本本身，而是“实验室拒收样本导致需要重新采样检测”这一事件。

13. **不能据此断定其他学生没有收到反馈**。  
    *逻辑边界解析*：限制性定语从句 `who submitted the revised report before Friday` 仅界定了本句作出肯定断言的子集合（“周五前交修订报告的学生收到了详细反馈”）。至于“周五之后交报告或未交修订版报告的学生是否收到了反馈”，原句并未包含排他算子 `Only`，因此属于**未确定信息（Unstated）**，阅读理解中绝不可将其自动反推为“其他学生一律没收到反馈”。

14. `that` 引导的是**限制性定语从句（Relative Clause）**，在从句中充当及物动词 `submitted` 的**直接宾语**。  
    *解析*：还原从句论元结构为 `the research team submitted [the proposal] last month`——因从句谓语 `submitted` 缺少宾语，故 `that` 是指代 `The proposal` 的关系代词（此处未揭示提案的具体内容，仅指明是“研究团队上月提交的那份提案”）。

15. `that` 引导的是**同位语从句（Appositive Clause）**，在从句中**不充当任何主语、宾语或状语成分**。  
    *解析*：从句 `the university should establish a public data archive` 主语、谓语、宾语完全齐备，直接等价展开说明外壳名词 `The proposal` 的具体提议内容，从属连词 `that` 仅承担“概念打包与连接”功能。

#### 5.9.1.4 第 16—20 题（名词化解压与关系动词强度）

16. **解压还原**：`Public funding was withdrawn rapidly, and this weakened the stability of community health services.`  
    *关系动词解析*：主语名词化 `The rapid withdrawal of public funding` 解压为被动变化“公共资金被迅速撤出”；加粗动词 **`undermined`** 属于“**削弱或瓦解**”类关系动词，表示对社区医疗服务的稳定性造成了渐进式破坏。

17. **解压还原**：`Because the platform failed to verify user identities, a wave of fraudulent transactions broke out suddenly.`  
    *关系动词解析*：主语 `The failure of the platform to verify...` 解压为“平台未能核验用户身份”；加粗动词 **`precipitated`** 属于“**诱发或催化**（急剧引发）”类强因果动词，强调直接促成了欺诈交易浪潮的突然爆发。

18. **解压还原**：`Because the virus continued to mutate, the diagnostic procedure needed to be redesigned.`  
    *关系动词解析*：主语 `The persistent mutation of the virus` 解压为“病毒持续发生变异”；加粗动词 **`necessitated`** 属于“**迫使或要求**（使……成为必要）”类关系动词。注意它只表明“客观上必须重新设计诊断流程”，不能直接等同于“已经完成了重新设计”。

19. **解压还原**：`After an automatic monitoring system was installed, daily manual inspections were no longer necessary.`  
    *关系动词解析*：主语 `The installation of...` 解压为“安装了自动监测系统”；加粗动词 **`obviated`** 属于“**消除需要**”类关系动词（`obviate the need for...` = 使……不再有必要）。

20. **解压还原**：`Because severe data loss occurred, a reliable comparison between the two groups could not be made.`  
    *关系动词解析*：主语 `Severe data loss` 解压为“发生了严重的数据丢失”；加粗动词 **`precluded`** 属于“**阻断或排除**”类关系动词，表示彻底排除了在两组之间进行可靠比较的可能性。

#### 5.9.1.5 第 21—36 题

21. **B**。  
    *解析*：首句通过 `Although..., their effectiveness depends heavily on the quality of the data...` 确立全段核心论点——数据质量会制约自适应学习平台的教学效果。A 项“必然降低”过于绝对；C 项与原文“减少有益挑战机会”相悖；D 项与末句 `little improvement despite high completion rates` 直接相反。

22. 指代 **`adaptive learning platforms`**（自适应学习平台）。  
    *解析*：在让步从句与主句构成的主从复合句中，主句主语中的物主代词 `their` 回指前文 `Although` 从句的主语 `adaptive learning platforms`。

23. **因果链**：`incomplete records`（学生数据记录不完整）→ `interpret temporary mistakes as lasting weaknesses`（软件将偶然失误误判为长期薄弱点）→ `repeatedly assigning basic tasks`（反复派发基础练习）→ `reduced opportunities for productive struggle`（有能力的学生丧失了适度挑战与提升的机会）。

24. **C（有条件、审慎）**。  
    *解析*：作者在首句用 `can adjust...` 承认了平台的技术调节能力，但随即指出其高度依赖数据质量，并用 `may explain` 审慎分析负面案例成因，体现了客观、有条件的学术审慎态度。

25. **要求公布项目全生命周期碳排放量的估算值**（`publish estimates of their lifetime carbon emissions`）。  
    *解析*：由首句方式状语 `By requiring large construction projects to publish estimates of their lifetime carbon emissions` 直接提取。

26. **B**。  
    *解析*：原文第二句明确指出 `The rule does not prohibit carbon-intensive development`（该规定并不禁止高碳开发），B 项“所有高碳项目都将被法律禁止”与原文直接矛盾。A、C、D 均可在原文找到对应依据。

27. `It` 指代 **`the new regulation / the rule`**（这项新规定）；助动词 `does` 位于实义动词原形 `increase` 之前，起**语篇强调与转折对比（Emphatic Affirmation）**作用，强调“尽管它不直接禁止、也不保证撤资，但它**确实**增加了言行不一企业的声誉与财务压力”。

28. **边界概括**：这项规定能够提高隐性环境成本对投资者的可见度，并增加言行不一企业的声誉与财务压力；但该规定**没有设置**禁止高碳开发的法律禁令，也**不能保证**投资者一定会从此类项目中撤资。

29. **实验对比与解释**：学后立即测试（`immediate recall test`）中，噪声组与安静组几乎没有可测量的差异；但延迟 48 小时后（`After a delay of forty-eight hours`），噪声组记住的单词明显少于安静组。这支持了“背景噪声对最初编码干扰较小，而主要干扰记忆的后期稳定（巩固）阶段”这一解释，但该机制仍需脑神经活动的直接测量来证实。

30. **C（后期稳定）**。  
    *解析*：原文 `distraction may interfere less with initial encoding than with the later stabilization of memory` 使用 `less with A than with B` 比较结构，明确指出干扰更可能影响 B（`the later stabilization of memory`，记忆的后期稳定），而非 A（`initial encoding`，最初编码）。

31. `a hypothesis` 作为总结性同位语，概括的是前文紧邻的推论命题：**`distraction may interfere less with initial encoding than with the later stabilization of memory`**（背景干扰对记忆最初编码的影响较小，而对记忆后期稳定阶段的影响较大）。

32. **证据强度边界**：原文使用了情态动词 `may`（可能），并在句末通过同位语明确指出 `a hypothesis that requires confirmation through direct measures of neural activity`（这一假说仍需通过直接测量神经活动加以证实）。若改写为“已经被证明（has been proved）”，则将尚待验证的科学假说越界夸大为既定事实。

33. **结构解析**：`What critics find most troubling about automated hiring systems` 是 `What` 引导的主语从句；系动词 `is` 后由 `not simply that... but that...` 引导两个并列的**表语从句**，遵循“轻前重后、先次要后核心”的聚焦原则，将批评者最担忧的核心锁定在第二个 `that` 从句上。

34. **C（受影响者难以了解并质疑拒绝标准）**。  
    *解析*：在 `not simply that A, but that B` 对比焦点结构中，核心落点在 `but that` 之后的 B：`the criteria by which candidates are rejected often remain inaccessible to those affected`（淘汰候选人的评判标准往往对受影响者不透明，使其无法识别事实错误或质疑无关假设）。

35. **否定条件关系**（`Without a clear account...` 等价于 `If there is no clear account...`）。缺少这一透明说明条件，直接限制了求职者两项核心权利能力：① **识别事实性错误**（`identify factual errors`）；② **质疑不相关的假设**（`challenge irrelevant assumptions`）。

36. **C（透明度是有效审查的必要条件，但单靠透明度不能消除歧视）**。  
    *解析*：原文末句 `Transparency alone will not eliminate discrimination, but it is a necessary condition for meaningful review` 严格区分了充分条件与必要条件：单靠透明度不足以消除歧视（非充分条件），但它是开展有效审查必不可少的前提（必要条件）。

---

### 5.9.2 进阶解码与句子加工消歧参考答案（第 37—82 题）

#### 5.9.2.1 第 37—41 题（进阶 D0—D3 分层）

37. **分层切分**：D0 `Although remote work has broadened access to urban jobs` / D1 `the uneven availability of reliable internet in rural communities` / D2 `has prevented some employees from taking full advantage of these opportunities` / D3 `reinforcing existing regional inequalities`。  
    *解码解析*：D0 为 `Although` 让步从句；D1 主语中心词是不可数抽象名词 **`availability`**（而非紧挨谓语的复数名词 `communities`，故谓语用单数 `has prevented`）；D2 核心谓语构式为 `prevent sb from doing sth`；D3 用现在分词短语 `reinforcing...` 引出“加剧既有地区不平等”的宏观后果。

38. **分层切分**：D0 `Following several weeks of overcrowding` / D1 `the decision by regional hospitals to share spare-bed data through a single platform` / D2 `enabled emergency coordinators to redirect patients more efficiently` / **无独立 D3**。  
    *解码解析*：D0 由分词型介词 `Following` 引出前期拥挤背景；D1 主语中心词为 **`decision`**（后接施事者 `by regional hospitals` 与内容不定式 `to share...`）；D2 为核心致使谓语 `enabled sb to do sth`，句尾副词 `more efficiently` 属于不定式内部状语，不单独划为 D3。

39. **分层切分**：D0 `Because estimates of long-term maintenance costs remain uncertain` / D1 `the recommendation that the city delay construction until additional soil tests are completed` / D2 `has gained support among local residents` / **无独立 D3**。  
    *解码解析*：D0 为 `Because` 原因状语从句；D1 主语中心词为 **`recommendation`**，其后紧接 `that` 引导的**同位语从句**（从句内部使用虚拟语气原形 `(should) delay`，并内嵌 `until` 时间从句）说明建议的具体内容；D2 谓语为 `has gained support among local residents`。

40. **分层切分**：**无独立 D0** / D1 `What began as a small volunteer project to digitize family records` / D2 `has evolved into a national archive` / D3 `giving researchers access to documents that might otherwise have been lost`。  
    *解码解析*：全句以 `What` 引导的名词性主语从句直接充当 D1 起点；D2 主干谓语为 `has evolved into a national archive`；D3 为现在分词结果状语 `giving researchers access to...`（内嵌含虚拟语气 `might otherwise have been lost` 的定语从句）。

41. **分层切分**：D0 `Even after extensive user testing` / D1 `the failure of the revised labeling system to distinguish clearly between similar medications` / D2 `continued to confuse hospital staff` / D3 `a problem likely to require more than a change in color`。  
    *解码解析*：D0 为让步时间介词短语；D1 主语中心词为 **`failure`**（后接主体 `of the revised labeling system` 与不定式补语 `to distinguish...`）；D2 核心谓语为 `continued to confuse hospital staff`；D3 为**总结性同位语** `a problem likely to require...`，对前文整个混淆问题作出严重性评估。

#### 5.9.2.2 第 42—46 题（中心词、谓语与主谓一致）

42. **完整主语**：`The proportion of imported fruit rejected at inspection points near the border`；**中心词**：单数名词 **`proportion`** → 选 **`has`**。  
    *解析*：过去分词短语 `rejected at inspection points near the border` 修饰 `imported fruit`；复数名词 `points` 等均为内部干扰词，真正统领谓语的是单数中心词 `proportion`（比例）。

43. **完整主语**：`The reliability of the predictions generated by the new climate models`；**中心词**：单数不可数名词 **`reliability`** → 选 **`depends`**。  
    *解析*：穿透介词短语 `of the predictions` 与过去分词短语 `generated by the new climate models`，排除复数干扰词 `predictions` 和 `models`，锁定单数核心词 `reliability`（可靠性）。

44. **完整主语**：`A number of students from the two evening classes` → 选复数助动词 **`have`**。  
    *解析*：固定量化构式 **`A number of + 复数名词`** 意为“许多/若干名学生”（相当于 `Many students`），中心语义落在复数名词 **`students`** 上，故谓语必用复数 `have`。

45. **完整主语**：`The number of complaints received by customer service departments across the region`；**中心词**：单数名词 **`number`** → 选单数助动词 **`has`**。  
    *解析*：与上一题形成经典最小对立体——**`The number of + 复数名词`** 意为“……的具体数量”，语法与语义中心词是单数名词 **`number`**（数量翻倍了），必须穿透复数干扰词 `complaints` 和 `departments` 选单数 `has`。

46. **完整主语**：`What the two independent investigations reveal about the safety system`（`What` 主语从句）→ 选单数系动词 **`is`**。  
    *解析*：整个 `What` 从句打包指代“两项独立调查所揭示的事实内容”，作为单一抽象概念統领单数系动词 `is`；从句内部主语 `the two independent investigations` 只控制从句谓语 `reveal`，不能控制主句系动词。

#### 5.9.2.3 第 47—51 题（从句边界、锚点与指代）

47. `which` 指代先行词名词短语 **`its east wing`**（博物馆的东翼展区）。  
    *解析*：从句谓语 `contained several rooms without modern fire alarms` 描述的是建筑空间 `east wing` 的内部硬件状况。

48. `which` 指代前面**整个主句事件**（“博物馆关闭东翼 `The museum closed its east wing`”这一行动）。  
    *与第 47 题的区别*：第 47 题的 `which` 修饰局部场所名词 `east wing`；而第 48 题中，迫使主办方推迟展览（`forced the organizers to postpone the exhibition`）的是“关闭东翼这一突发事件”。

49. **不能据此断定其他居民未获准入场**。  
    *逻辑边界解析*：原句 `Residents who registered before noon were admitted to the public hearing` 仅确认了“中午前登记的居民获准入场”这一正面事实；原句并未使用排他词 `Only`，因此对于“中午后现场登记或未提前登记的居民是否也能入场”并未作出否定界定。

50. `that` 引导的是**限制性定语从句（Relative Clause）**，在从句中充当及物动词 `investigated` 的**宾语**（即 `the journalist investigated the claim for six months`）。

51. `that` 引导的是**同位语从句（Appositive Clause）**，在从句中**不充当主语或宾语**。  
    *解析*：从句 `several inspection records had been altered`（若干检查记录遭到篡改）主谓完整，直接揭示外壳名词 `The claim` 的具体指控内容，`that` 仅作连接词。

#### 5.9.2.4 第 52—56 题（名词化解压、动词关系与强度）

52. **解压还原**：`Because safety standards were enforced inconsistently, public confidence in the inspection system was weakened.`  
    *关系动词判定*：**`undermined`** 属于“**削弱或瓦解**”类关系动词，连接主语“安全标准执行不一致”与宾语“公众对检查制度的信心”。

53. **解压还原**：`Several key suppliers were suddenly removed, and this directly triggered a severe shortage of basic materials.`  
    *关系动词判定*：**`precipitated`** 属于“**诱发或催化**（突发促成）”类强因果关系动词，凸显关键供应商被突然移除后迅速引发原材料严重短缺。

54. **解压还原**：`Because two production lines were contaminated, the affected products needed to be recalled immediately.`  
    *关系动词判定*：**`necessitated`** 属于“**迫使或要求**（使……成为必要）”类关系动词（注意：仅表达客观上必须立即召回，不自动等同于召回动作已经执行完毕）。

55. **解压还原**：`After the application process was digitized, applicants no longer needed to submit paper copies in person.`  
    *关系动词判定*：**`obviated`** 属于“**消除需要**”类关系动词（`obviate the need for...` = 免除……的必要）。

56. **解压还原**：`Because there was no comparable control group, a firm conclusion about the treatment's effectiveness could not be drawn.`  
    *关系动词判定*：**`precluded`** 属于“**阻断或排除**”类关系动词，表示缺乏可比对照组彻底排除了得出确定性药效结论的可能。

#### 5.9.2.5 第 57—72 题（高阶学术段落深度阅读四篇）

57. **C（短信提醒能减少因遗忘造成的爽约，但效果受到实际安排障碍的限制）**。  
    *解析*：首句通过 `Although..., the effect was much smaller among patients whose work schedules changed...` 指出短信提醒的群体局限性，末句 `caution that reminders can reduce missed visits caused by forgetfulness, but cannot by themselves remove practical barriers to rescheduling` 完整收束全段主旨。A 项“同样有效”、B 项“导致忘记”、D 项“取消所有预约”均与原文相悖。

58. **工作时间每周变动不定的患者**（`patients whose work schedules changed from week to week`）。

59. **完整因果链**：患者工作排班每周变动（`work schedules changed from week to week`）→ 诊所短信在固定时间发送（`messages were sent at fixed times`）→ 短信送达时患者往往无法查看手机或联系诊所（`arrived when these patients were unable to check their phones or contact the clinic`）→ 存在实际改期障碍，导致提高到诊率的效果大打折扣。

60. **证据边界解析**：试验首月到诊率总体确实有所上升（`increased attendance... during the first month`），且研究者明确肯定短信提醒能够有效减少**因单纯遗忘**（`caused by forgetfulness`）造成的爽约。原文指出的是“单靠短信提醒无法消除实际改期障碍（`cannot by themselves remove practical barriers`）”，承认工具的局限性绝不等于全盘否定其既有功效。

61. **提供财政补贴（`By offering grants for reflective roofs`）**，以此抵消此前阻碍业主安装反射屋顶的高昂前期成本（`upfront cost`）。

62. **B（所有租户都会直接获得较低的能源费用）**。  
    *解析*：原文第二句明确指出 `The program does not require landlords to pass energy savings on to tenants, nor does it reach renters whose building owners choose not to apply`（既不强制房东把省下的电费让利给租客，也无法惠及房东不申请补贴的租客），故 B 项绝对化推断完全错误。

63. `It` 指代 **`the program`**（这项反射屋顶补贴计划）；助动词 `does` 位于动词原形 `create` 前起**强调肯定与转折对比（Emphatic Contrast）**作用，强调“尽管该计划有上述两项局限，但它**确实**为业主创造了可衡量的安装激励”。

64. **政策边界概括**：该补贴计划能够有效激励自愿参与的房产主克服前期成本障碍、安装反射屋顶并降低建筑表面温度；但它**不能强制**房东将节省的能源开支转交租户，也**无法覆盖**业主未申请该项目的租房群体。

65. **时间维度结果反转**：在阅读纠错信息当天的即时测试中（`conducted the same day`），绝大多数志愿者都能准确识别出误导性标题的虚假声明；然而**一周之后（`One week later`）**，那些曾多次接触过误导性标题的人，反而比只见过一次的人更容易信以为真（即便两组读过完全相同的辟谣纠错信息）。

66. 选 **B（重复接触带来的熟悉感可能比对纠正信息的记忆更持久）**。  
    *干扰项诊断*：原文为 `repetition may create a sense of familiarity that outlasts memory for the correction, an explanation that still needs to be tested...`。A 项颠倒了 `outlasts`（比……更持久）的主客体关系；C 项将 `may / still needs to be tested` 的假说篡改为“研究已证实”；D 项将“一周后（`One week later`）”的延迟效应错误移花接木到了“当天测试”。

67. 总结性同位语 `an explanation` 概括的是紧邻的前一判断：**`repetition may create a sense of familiarity that outlasts memory for the correction`**（重复接触虚假标题所产生的熟悉感，其持续时间可能超过人们对辟谣纠正信息的记忆）。

68. **证据强度边界**：原文使用了认识情态动词 `suggest that repetition may...`，并在句末通过同位语明确限定 `an explanation that still needs to be tested under conditions in which exposure frequency is carefully controlled`（该解释仍需在严格控制接触频率的条件下加以检验）。因此这只是一项待验证的机制假说，不能越界写成“已经被证明（has been proved）”。

69. **并列对比聚焦关系（`not that A, but that B`）**：两个 `that` 从句共同充当 `What` 主语从句系动词 `is` 后的并列表语从句。第一个 `not that...` 排除次要或被误解的原因（并非反对算法参与决策本身），第二个 `but that...` 锁定临床医生真正强烈反对的核心症结。

70. **C（不确定病例可能在缺乏证据说明的情况下被判为低优先级）**。  
    *解析*：直接对应 `but that` 后的核心表语从句：`uncertain cases can be assigned a low priority without any explanation of the evidence used`。

71. **`Unless` 表达排他性否定条件关系（必要条件，等价于 `If the interface does not show...`）**。一旦缺少“界面展示哪些观察证据促成了该分诊建议”这一透明条件，医务人员将丧失两项关键临床把关能力：① **无法修正缺失的数据**（`cannot correct missing data`）；② **无法判断某个罕见异常症状是否被算法忽略**（`judge whether an unusual symptom was ignored`）。

72. **B（提供解释对审查必要，但不能保证每项建议都可靠）**。  
    *解析*：末句 `Explanations will not make every automated recommendation reliable, but they are necessary for responsible clinical review` 精准界定了“非充分条件（不能保证每条建议都可靠）”与“必要条件（是负责任临床复核的必备前提）”。

#### 5.9.2.6 第 73—77 题（句子加工、花园路径消歧与工作记忆）

**73.** **双重中心嵌套（Center-Embedding）三层主谓宾剥离**：
- **全句主干（D1 + D2）**：**`The report was published yesterday.`**（主语 `The report`，被动谓语 `was published`）；
- **第一层定语从句（修饰 `The report`）**：**`the analyst revised the report`**（主语 `the analyst`，谓语 `revised`，宾语为关系代词 `that` 指代的 `the report`）；
- **第二层定语从句（嵌套在第一层内部，修饰 `the analyst`）**：**`Maya hired the analyst`**（主语 `Maya`，谓语 `hired`，宾语为关系代词 `whom` 指代的 `the analyst`）。

*解析*：该句将两个定语从句层层塞在主语 `The report` 内部，导致句末连续堆叠三个动词 `hired revised was published`。解码秘诀是“**由内向外配对消消乐**”：最里面的 `Maya` 配第一个动词 `hired`；中间的 `the analyst` 配第二个动词 `revised`；最外层句首的 `The report` 配第三个谓语 `was published`。

**74.** **线性拆句消解工作记忆过载**：
- 示范改写一（三句顺承线性链）：**`Maya hired an analyst. The analyst revised the report, and it was published yesterday.`**
- 示范改写二（被动后置右分支单句）：**`The report revised by the analyst whom Maya had hired was published yesterday.`**

*解析*：英语工作记忆最怕“名词1 + 名词2 + 名词3 + 动词3 + 动词2 + 动词1”的中心嵌套。通过拆成线性小句或将其中一层改写为右分支过去分词 `revised by...`，即可彻底消除回读负担。

**75.** **花园路径句（Garden-Path Sentence）标点消歧**：
- **加标点修正**：**`While Maya was reading, the report fell off the desk.`**
- **暂时性误读机制（Early Closure Fallacy）**：因为动词 `read` 既可作及物动词也可作不及物动词，若不在 `reading` 后加逗号，人类大脑的阅读解析器遵循“迟关闭/就近吸纳原则”，会本能地把紧随其后的名词短语 `the report` 当作 `was reading` 的**宾语**（误读为“当 Maya 正在读那份报告时……”）；直到撞上后面的限定动词 `fell off`，才发现 `fell` 没有主语，被迫退回重读，把 `the report` 重新解析为主句主语。在句首状语从句末加上逗号，正是阻断此类花园路径误读的视觉路标。

**76.** **句子词数与加工难度的认知辨析**：该判断**错误**（45 词的句子绝不一定比 20 词的句子更难加工）。
- **决定加工负荷的核心句法因素（任列两项即可）**：
  1. **分支方向与嵌套深度（Branching Direction & Center-Embedding）**：一个 45 词但严格按右分支铺排的累积句（主干在前、修饰语顺次挂在句尾），远比一个只有 20 词却包含双重中心嵌套（如第 73 题）或左分支长前置定语的句子更容易读懂；
  2. **主谓依存距离（Dependency Distance）**：主语中心词与限定谓语动词之间相隔的词数越长，工作记忆维持主语激活的成本就越高；
  3. **结构歧义与花园路径陷阱（Ambiguity / Garden-Pathing）**：省略关系词或标点缺失导致的局部歧义（如过去分词与过去式同形 `The horse raced past the barn fell`）会引发高昂的回读重解析成本；
  4. **词汇抽象度与多重否定辖域（Lexical Abstractness & Multiple Negation）**。

**77.** **阅读主干提取中的模态与条件保真**：
- **丢失的关键信息**：丢失了 `If` 引导的**触发条件前提**（`If the pressure rises` 压力上升时）以及情态动词 `may` 标示的**可能性边界**（可能故障 $\neq$ 已经/必然故障）。
- **正确精简概括**：**“若压力上升，水泵可能会发生故障。”**

*解析*：阅读长难句提炼“主干”时，删去的是次要修饰细节，但**否定词（`not / never / little`）、条件前提（`if / unless`）与情态认识强度（`may / might / could`）属于决定命题真值的逻辑算子**，一旦删掉就会把“有条件的潜在风险”篡改为“无条件的既定事实”。

---

#### 5.9.2.7 句子加工与花园幽径消歧进阶参考答案（第 78—82 题）

78. **早期闭合与及物性陷阱消歧**：
- 假性解读：读到 `allegations of fraud` 时，及物动词 `was investigating` 天然吸纳其充当直接宾语（误读为“当委员会调查欺诈指控时”）；
- 碰撞词：后方的谓语动词 **`emerged`**。读到此处时发现 `emerged` 无主可用，引发认知冲突；
- 消歧改写：`While the committee was investigating, [the allegations of fraud] emerged in the national press.`（加逗号切分状语从句边界，还原后方完整主谓结构）。

79. **缩略被动分词识别与显性还原**：
- 误读根源：动词过去式与过去分词同形。大脑按照“主语+主动谓语”最小依附原则，将 `molecules synthesized` 顺理成章解读为主谓动作（分子合成了……）；直至读到后方的 `failed to bind`，才惊觉 `failed` 才是真正的主句谓语。
- 显性还原版：`The synthetic molecules [which were synthesized by the automated biochemical laboratory] failed to bind to target receptors under ambient temperature.`（合成分子未能与靶受体结合）。

80. **引导词 that 省略与论元跃迁**：
- 论元跃迁名词：`the critical foundation flaw`（地基缺陷）。在初读阶段被识别为及物动词 `recognized` 的直接宾语；读到后方情态谓语 `could lead to` 时，被迫跃迁为宾语从句的语法主语。
- 写作建议：在正式学术论文中，当宾语从句主语较长或携带后置定语时，**强制保留连接词 `that`**（`recognized that the critical foundation flaw could...`），为读者大脑提供清晰的语法边界导航，消除回读成本。

81. **双及物动词误接与两步认知修订**：
- 误读机制：动词 `promise` 常接双宾语（`promise sb sth`），读者容易把 `the junior scholars` 认作承受承诺的间接宾语；
- 两步修订过程：① 读到定语从句 `who arrived late` 时，继续将先行词作为间接宾语保持在记忆槽中；② 读到谓语动词 `would receive` 时，推翻前置双宾语假设，将 `the junior scholars...` 整体判定为从句主语；规范写作中应补上 `that`：`promised that the junior scholars...`。

82. **Gibson DLT 工作记忆负荷量化分析**：
- 成本对比：版本 A 属于多层中心嵌套（Center-Embedding）。主语 `The report` 必须在工作记忆中苦等谓语 `was approved`，中间跨越了 `the analyst` 和 `the committee` 两个新的论元小句，存储成本与整合成本极高，容易导致大脑工作记忆栈溢出；版本 B 将依存关系切解为前向推进的线性叙事，大幅缩短了修饰语与中心词的依存距离。
- 参考译文：“委员会昨天任命了一位分析师，该分析师起草了一份报告并获得了批准。”

### 5.9.3 读写转化与高阶右分支综合改写参考答案（第 83—130 题）

#### 5.9.3.1 第 83—88 题（GPS 场景定位）

83. `The school will hold a science exhibition on the first floor of the new library next Friday afternoon to showcase students' innovative projects.`  
    *解析*：主干 `The school will hold a science exhibition` 后依次挂接地点（先具体楼层 `on the first floor` 后建筑 `of the new library`）、时间（`next Friday afternoon`）与目的不定式（`to showcase...`）。

84. `The volunteers distributed food packages at the temporary shelter by the river early on the morning after the storm, with the assistance of local doctors.`  
    *解析*：主干 `The volunteers distributed food packages` 后先接地点（`at the temporary shelter by the river`），再接时间（`early on the morning after the storm`），句末用介词短语 `with the assistance of local doctors` 补充协作方式。

85. `After three years of fieldwork, the researchers released the report at an international conference to promote the protection of wetlands.`  
    *解析*：将前期时间跨度 `After three years of fieldwork` 置于句首 D0 槽位；主干后接会议地点 `at an international conference`，句末以目的不定式 `to promote the protection of wetlands` 收束。

86. `The boy waited for the rescue team in the dark cave throughout the night, his arms wrapped tightly around his knees.`  
    *解析*：主干 `The boy waited for the rescue team` 后接空间容器 `in the dark cave` 与持续时间 `throughout the night`，句末挂载“名词 + 过去分词”独立主格结构 `his arms wrapped tightly around his knees` 刻画无助寒冷的肢体细节。

87. `Facing sustained public criticism, the committee revised the policy at an emergency meeting on Monday to protect the rights of temporary workers.`  
    *解析*：句首用现在分词短语 `Facing sustained public criticism` 交代背景压力（逻辑主语为 `the committee`）；主干后依次接地点、时间与目的不定式 `to protect...`。

88. `At dusk, the old woman opened the letter beside the fireplace, her fingers trembling slightly with excitement.`  
    *解析*：句首 `At dusk` 设定黄昏时间氛围；主干 `opened the letter` 后接位置 `beside the fireplace`；句末用独立主格 `her fingers trembling slightly with excitement` 特写手部微颤，外化激动情绪。

#### 5.9.3.2 第 89—94 题（定语从句与信息嫁接）

89. `Only the students who completed the field survey will present their findings tomorrow.`  
    *解析*：使用限制性定语从句 `who completed the field survey` 界定允许汇报的学生范围，与 `Only` 配合构成排他性限定，不加逗号。

90. `Professor Lin, who has studied rural education for twenty years, will deliver the opening speech.`  
    *解析*：先行词 `Professor Lin` 指称唯一确定，用双逗号隔开的非限制性定语从句 `who has studied...` 补充介绍其学术背景。

91. `The city restored the old theater, which attracted new visitors to the district.`  
    *解析*：句末 `, which...` 引导非限制性定语从句，`which` 概括前面“市政府修缮老剧院”这一整件事，说明该举措带来的积极效应。

92. `We visited a village where many traditional wooden houses have survived.`  
    *解析*：先行词 `a village` 在从句中充当地点状语（`in the village`），故使用关系副词 `where`（或 `in which`）引导限制性定语从句。

93. `The laboratory developed a low-cost sensor that can detect unsafe levels of lead in drinking water.`  
    *解析*：在先行词 `a low-cost sensor` 后挂接 `that / which` 引导的限制性定语从句，关系代词在从句中作主语，说明传感器的核心检测功能。

94. `The museum has launched an online archive through which users can examine rare manuscripts.`  
    *解析*：运用**介词前置**（Pied-Piping）构式 `through which`（= `users can examine rare manuscripts through the online archive`），衔接严密且符合正式书面语规范。

#### 5.9.3.3 第 95—100 题（概念包裹与关系信号灯）

95. 主语位置：`That daily exercise improves concentration is supported by growing evidence.`  
    宾语位置：`Many teachers recognize that daily exercise improves concentration.`  
    表语位置：`The central claim is that daily exercise improves concentration.`  
    名词后的内容说明位置：`The claim that daily exercise improves concentration deserves careful examination.`  
    *解析*：展示同一核心命题通过 `that` 概念打包后，在名词性从句四大核心槽位（主语、及物动词宾语、系动词表语、外壳名词 `claim` 同位语）中的灵活迁移。

96. 主语从句：`Why the experiment failed remains unclear.`  
    宾语从句：`The researchers are investigating why the experiment failed.`  
    *解析*：用连接副词 `why` 打包原因疑问命题，从句内部严格保持陈述语序 `the experiment failed`。

97. 前置：`Although the evidence remains incomplete, the committee approved a limited pilot program.`  
    后置：`The committee approved a limited pilot program although the evidence remains incomplete.`  
    *解析*：`Although` 引导让步状语从句，前置时用逗号分隔以凸显主句决策，后置时补充说明让步背景。

98. `As the river level continued to rise, the emergency team evacuated the village.`  
    *解析*：`As` 在此可兼表伴随时间推移与原因；也可明确用 `because` 表因果，或当仅陈述先后/共现事实时用 `when`，体现连接词对事件逻辑关系的精确标示。

99. `While the new timetable saves commuting time, some students still prefer the old schedule.`  
    *解析*：句首 `While` 表让步与对比，将新课表“节省通勤时间”的优势与部分学生“仍偏爱旧课表”的现状并置。

100. `Because the sample was too small, the researchers avoided a firm conclusion.`（或后置：`The researchers avoided a firm conclusion because the sample was too small.`）  
    *解析*：用从属连词 `Because` 建立样本量过小与避免下绝对结论之间的因果关联，消除原句无连接词的松散并列。

#### 5.9.3.4 第 101—106 题（介词修饰链、名词化与关系动词）

101. `the decline in manufacturing output across three coastal provinces over the past five years`  
    *解析*：中心词 `the decline` 后依次挂载：下降指标 `in manufacturing output` → 地理范围 `across three coastal provinces` → 时间跨度 `over the past five years`。

102. `the debate over the ethical use of facial recognition in public schools across several European countries`  
    *解析*：中心词 `the debate` 后逐层右向延展：争论焦点 `over the ethical use` → 技术对象 `of facial recognition` → 应用场所 `in public schools` → 宏观区域 `across several European countries`。

103. `The rapid expansion of the city caused a substantial decline in wetland area.`  
    *解析*：将两个动态变化名词化为 `The rapid expansion of the city` 和 `a substantial decline in wetland area`，并用中性因果动词 `caused`（或 `led to`）连接，严格忠实于原题证据强度（不随意夸大为突发 `precipitated` 或削弱为部分促因 `contributed to`）。

104. `The researchers' failure to share the raw data precluded independent verification.`  
    *解析*：将否定原因名词化为 `The researchers' failure to share the raw data`，搭配阻断关系动词 `precluded`（排除……的可能）连接宾语 `independent verification`。

105. `The rapid increase in fuel prices placed severe financial pressure on small transport companies.`  
    *解析*：主语名词化为 `The rapid increase in fuel prices`，谓语采用 `placed severe financial pressure on...` 精准表达成本上涨对小型运输企业造成的财务挤压。

106. `Existing research suggests that social media use may be associated with insufficient sleep among adolescents.`  
    *解析*：运用 `suggests` 与 `may be associated with`（可能与……相关）进行审慎学术表达（Hedging），严守“相关性不等于已证实因果”的科学论证红线。

#### 5.9.3.5 第 107—110 题（综合改写第一组：非谓语动作链）

107. `Hearing a child call for help, Maya dropped her bag and ran toward the river, searching the dark water for any sign of movement.`  
    *解析*：句首现在分词 `Hearing...` 交代触发感知，主句并列谓语 `dropped... and ran...` 推进核心救援动作，句末现在分词 `searching...` 补充伴随搜寻动作，全句动作链一气呵成。

108. `Looking across the crowded hall, the speaker took a slow breath, unfolded his notes, and began the presentation calmly.`  
    *解析*：句首现在分词短语 `Looking across the crowded hall` 铺垫视线动作，主句用三个顺承动词 `took..., unfolded..., and began...` 细腻展现演讲者登台时的从容节奏。

109. `The dog had been trapped under the fallen fence. It struggled to its feet and followed the firefighter toward safety.`  
    *解析*：原题仅交代“曾被困”与“挣扎站起跟随消防员”，保留两句可避免凭空捏造救援过程；若上下文已明确狗被解救，则可用过去分词短语置首：`Freed from beneath the fallen fence, the dog struggled to its feet...`（`Freed` 的受事逻辑主语为 `the dog`）。

110. `The rain striking the windows and the lights flickering above us, we waited in silence for the power to return.`（或加 `With`：`With the rain striking the windows and the lights flickering above us, we waited...`）  
    *解析*：因 `the rain`、`the lights` 与主句主语 `we` 逻辑主语不同，故采用独立主格结构（或 `With` 复合结构）渲染停电时的风雨交加环境。

#### 5.9.3.6 第 111—114 题（综合改写第一组：同位语与绝对结构）

111. `The town converted an abandoned factory into a public library, a decision that gave young people a safe place to study.`  
    *解析*：句末使用总结性同位语 `, a decision that...` 将前面“废弃工厂改建为公共图书馆”的事件打包，并阐明其对青少年的社会价值。

112. `The medical team created a mobile clinic for remote villages, an initiative that greatly improved access to basic care.`  
    *解析*：用外壳名词 `an initiative` 作总结性同位语概括“建立流动诊所”这一创举，后接 `that` 定语从句说明其改善偏远地区医疗可达性的成效。

113. `The four teams examined different aspects of the problem, one analyzing costs, another studying public opinion, a third testing technical feasibility, and the last reviewing legal risks.`  
    *解析*：主句总述四个团队从不同维度切入，句末用四组并列的“代词 + 现在分词”独立主格结构分述各自职责，层次井然。

114. `Daniel stepped onto the stage and faced the audience, his heart pounding, his hands cold, the spotlight bright on his face.`  
    *解析*：句末连续挂载三个独立主格结构——`his heart pounding`（名词+现在分词）、`his hands cold`（名词+形容词）、`the spotlight bright on his face`（名词+形容词短语），多维定格登台瞬间的心跳、体感与强光特写。

#### 5.9.3.7 第 115—118 题（综合改写第一组：信息焦点与倒装）

115. `At the end of the forest path stood a narrow stone bridge.`  
    *解析*：方位介词短语 `At the end of the forest path` 前置触发完全倒装，动词 `stood` 置于主语 `a narrow stone bridge` 之前，实现视线由林间小径尽头向石桥的自然聚焦。

116. `Only after the project had failed twice did she understand the value of patience.`  
    *解析*：`Only + 时间状语从句` 置于句首，触发主句部分倒装（`did she understand`），突出“经历两次失败后才真正领悟”的深刻转折。

117. `Exhausting as the climb was, the children refused to turn back.`  
    *解析*：表语形容词 `Exhausting` 提至让步连词 `as` 之前构成让步倒装（等价于 `Although the climb was exhausting`），强化攀登的极度疲惫与孩子们坚持到底之间的张力。

118. 形式主语：`It was acknowledged by the researchers that the treatment could produce serious side effects in patients with weakened immune systems.`  
主动语态：`The researchers acknowledged that the treatment could produce serious side effects in patients with weakened immune systems.`  
本题主动语态通常更简洁；是否选择被动取决于上下文的信息衔接。

#### 5.9.3.8 第 119—122 题（综合改写第二组：非谓语动作链）

119. `Smelling smoke in the hallway, Elena alerted her neighbors, turned off the gas supply, and guided two children outside.`  
     *解析*：句首现在分词短语 `Smelling smoke in the hallway` 交代突发感知触发点，主句用三个顺承过去式动词 `alerted..., turned off..., and guided...` 构成干脆利落的应急行动链。

120. `Noticing a fallen branch ahead, the cyclist slowed down, signaled to the riders behind him, and moved carefully around the obstruction.`  
     *解析*：句首现在分词 `Noticing...` 表原因与时间起点，主句三连动 `slowed down, signaled..., and moved...` 完整呈现骑行者避障的连续动作。

121. `Closed for repairs after being damaged by the storm, the footbridge forced residents to take a two-kilometer detour to reach the market.`  
     *解析*：句首过去分词短语 `Closed for repairs after being damaged by the storm` 的受事逻辑主语为 `the footbridge`；主句选用致使关系动词 `forced` 将无生命主语 `the footbridge` 与居民绕行的结果自然连接。

122. `With the wind pushing against the sails and waves breaking over the deck, the crew continued to secure the damaged mast.`  
     *解析*：使用 `With + 名词 + 现在分词` 复合独立主格结构（`the wind pushing... and waves breaking...`）描绘狂风巨浪的外部险境，与主句船员加固受损桅杆的行动形成环境衬托。

#### 5.9.3.9 第 123—126 题（综合改写第二组：同位语与绝对结构）

123. `The council turned an empty parking lot into a public garden, a decision that created a cooler gathering place for nearby residents.`  
     *解析*：句末使用总结性同位语 `, a decision that...` 概括前句“将空置停车场改造成公共花园”的市政举措，并顺势引出其降温便民效果。

124. `The school introduced free breakfast for all pupils, an initiative that improved morning attendance.`  
     *解析*：用外壳名词 `an initiative` 作总结性同位语概括“推出免费早餐”这一新举措，后接 `that` 定语从句说明其对早间出勤率的提升作用。

125. `The three groups approached the issue differently, one interviewing residents, another analyzing traffic data, and the third mapping accident locations.`  
     *解析*：主句总述三组采取不同研究路径，句末用三组并列的“代词（`one / another / the third`）+ 现在分词”独立主格结构具体展开各组方法。

126. `Nora opened the email and read the result, her breath held, her fingers frozen above the touchpad, the room unusually quiet around her.`  
     *解析*：句末连续挂载三个独立主格结构：`her breath held`（名词+过去分词）、`her fingers frozen...`（名词+过去分词）、`the room unusually quiet...`（名词+形容词短语），从呼吸、手指到周围环境全方位营造屏息凝神的紧张瞬间。

#### 5.9.3.10 第 127—130 题（综合改写第二组：信息焦点与倒装）

127. `Beyond the harbor wall lay a row of small fishing boats.`  
     *解析*：方位介词短语 `Beyond the harbor wall` 前置触发完全倒装，姿态动词 `lay`（`lie` 的过去式）置于主语 `a row of small fishing boats` 之前，符合视觉由近及远的描写顺序。

128. `Only after they had examined the foundation did the engineers discover the source of the vibration.`  
     *解析*：`Only + 时间状语从句` 置于句首触发主句部分倒装（助动词 `did` 提至主语 `the engineers` 之前，动词还原为 `discover`），而 `after` 从句内部仍保持陈述语序。

129. `Incomplete as the evidence was, the pattern was difficult to dismiss.`  
     *解析*：将表语形容词 `Incomplete` 提至让步连词 `as`（或 `though`）之前构成让步前置倒装，强调“尽管证据尚不完整，但呈现出的规律难以忽视”。

130. 形式主语：`It was admitted by the committee that the new rule might unintentionally exclude applicants without stable internet access.`  
主动语态：`The committee admitted that the new rule might unintentionally exclude applicants without stable internet access.`  
主动语态通常更简洁。

### 5.9.4 篇章级阅读图谱与宏观论证树图（第 131—135 题）

**131.** ① 核心主张（Core Claim）：交互式 AI 系统实际上能够减轻二语学习者的外部工作记忆负荷，而非传统观念认为的引发认知萎缩（`interactive AI systems actually alleviate extraneous working-memory load`）；
② 并列支链论据（Sub-arguments）：
- 支链 A（路标词：`On the one hand`）：实时句法分析帮助初学者监控修饰依附歧义，且不消耗过多注意力资源；
- 支链 B（路标词：`On the other hand`）：自适应反馈促使学生对照语料库主动论证修改理由，激发元认知反思。

解析：学术段落的典型骨架是“宏观主张 + 并列支链展开”。通过抓取路标词（On the one hand / On the other hand），能够瞬间在脑海中生成树状分叉结构，阅读速度与理解精度成倍提升。

**132.** 三阶认知图谱：
- 步骤一·让步（Concession，标志词：`Admittedly`, `rightly note`）：承认自动化评分软件具有不可否认的批改规模与极高的成本效益（速度极快）；
- 步骤二·反驳（Rebuttal，标志词：`Nevertheless`, `fundamentally blind to`）：算法表面指标无法识别思想深度、情感真诚度与文化隐喻；
- 步骤三·修正立场（Refined Thesis，标志词：`Consequently`, `necessitating`）：纯依赖算法将误伤具有创造力的非标准表达，因此必须推行“人机协同评估”（既不能全盘否定 AI，也不能全盘抛弃人工批改）。
作者最终支持命题：必须采用保留不可替代的人工评估的“人机混合教学评估模型”（hybrid pedagogical model）。

解析：高阶学术与高考阅读中最具迷惑性的就是让步反驳。读懂段落的关键，是看准 `Nevertheless` 之后的反转焦点，绝不把作者的“让步客套话”当成作者的“真实立场”。

**133.** 级联因果链（Causal Cascade）：
- 节点 A（起始事件）：海表温度升高引发共生藻类的热应激（`Elevated sea-surface temperatures trigger thermal stress`）；
- 节点 B（中间机制）：引发热带珊瑚礁发生灾难性白化死亡，摧毁关键结构栖息地（`precipitating catastrophic coral bleaching... structural reef mortality`）；
- 节点 C（终极生态后果）：使数千种海洋生物丧失觅食与繁殖地，导致沿海生物多样性灾难性崩溃（`deprives organisms of habitats... collapse of coastal biodiversity`）。

解析：在科技与学术长难句中，因果链往往通过“名词化 + 动词分词（trigger → precipitating → resulting in）”层层递进。画出因果树图，就能把复杂的多重从句还原为清晰的线性事件驱动流。

**134.** ① 段落一超主位（Hyper-Theme）：以汽车为中心的传统城市交通规划因过度依赖公路扩张而陷入灾难性僵局（问题诊断）；
② 段落二超主位（Hyper-Theme）：摆脱该系统性危机的颠覆性方案在于快速推行公共交通导向的多模态出行（方案提供）；
③ 宏观推进图谱：两段之间构成了经典的“Systemic Problem / Crisis → Innovative Solution / Deployment”宏观语步衔接，段落二首句的 `A transformative resolution to this systemic crisis` 精准回扣段落一的 `impasse`，完成概念闭环。

解析：篇章级阅读要求读者跳出单个句子的微观语法，站在“段落超主位”的宏观高度审视全篇推进网络，识别体裁展开的全局意图。

**135.** Toulmin 论证骨架逆向解码：
- 主张（Claim）：广泛阅读真实文学作品能显著加速二语学习者的句法习得；
- 数据事实（Data/Grounds）：长期追踪表明，坚持阅读小说的学生产出的多小句右分支复杂句比纯教材组多出 40%；
- 正当理由（Warrant）：通过充满情感沉浸的丰富语言环境，复杂句法模式在大脑神经层面得以自然固化与同频共振；
- 反驳考量（Rebuttal）：尽管死记硬背支持者宣称明确规则教学见效更快，但这种机械知识在实时交际压力下无法维持自发生成。

解析：图尔敏模型（Toulmin Model）是西方学术写作与高阶阅读分析的核心工具。通过此种逆向解码，学生能真正看透长篇论证的肌理，完成从“被动阅读者”到“主动逻辑拆解者”的蜕变。

### 5.9.5 句法红线实战诊断参考答案（第 136—151 题）

#### 5.9.5.1 第 136—143 题（句法红线纠错第一组）

136. **错误类型：关系信号灯多余叠加（红线 5：`Although... but...` 双重连词冲突）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`Although the weather was extremely cold, the volunteers continued their search.`**（或删去 `Although` 保留 `, but...`）。  
     *解析*：英语一个复合句中，两分句之间只能由一个连接算子统领；从属连词 `Although` 已标示让步从属关系，不能再叠加并列转折连词 `but`。

137. **错误类型：悬空修饰语（红线 2：Dangling Modifier）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`Walking through the exhibition hall, I was attracted by the ancient paintings.`**（或将分词展开为显性时间状语从句：**`As I walked through the exhibition hall, the ancient paintings attracted my attention.`**）。  
     *解析*：句首现在分词短语 `Walking through the exhibition hall` 的隐含施事者是看展的人（`I`），而主句语法主语却是 `the ancient paintings`，导致语法上变成“古画自己在展厅里走路”。

138. **错误类型：远距离主谓单复数脱节（红线 3：Long-Distance Subject-Verb Disagreement）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The discovery of several previously unknown letters in the private collection has changed our understanding of the poet.`**  
     *解析*：主语的核心名词是句首的单数名词 **`discovery`**（发现），而非介词链内部的复数名词 `letters`，故现在完成时助动词必须由 `have` 改为单数 **`has`**。

139. **错误类型：非限制性定语从句误用 `that`（红线 6）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The city introduced a recycling program, which reduced household waste by nearly twenty percent.`**  
     *解析*：逗号后的非限制性定语从句（无论指代先行词 `program` 还是概括整个前句事件）严禁使用关系词 `that`，必须改为 **`which`**。

140. **错误类型：嵌入式疑问从句语序未还原（红线 7）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`I would like to know why the committee rejected our proposal.`**  
     *解析*：`why` 引导的从句嵌入 `know` 后充当宾语从句，不再是独立疑问句，必须解散借用的助动词 `did` 并将动词还原为过去式 `rejected`（恢复 `主语 + 谓语` 的陈述语序）。

141. **错误类型：`There be` 存在句后直接堆叠限定谓语动词（双谓语冲突）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`There are many students who believe that the library should remain open longer.`**（或直接删去冗余的 `There are`：**`Many students believe that the library should remain open longer.`**）。  
     *解析*：`There are many students` 已经拥有主句限定谓语 `are`，其后若要继续描述 `students` 的动作，必须使用定语从句 `who believe...` 或非谓语分词 `believing...`。

142. **错误类型：中式长前置修饰堆叠（红线 1：Left-Branching Trap）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The plan announced yesterday for the construction of a community health center near the railway station received public support.`**  
     *解析*：原句将 `yesterday announced` 和介词短语 `near the railway station` 硬塞在中心名词 `plan` 与 `center` 之前；应将过去分词短语 `announced yesterday` 和地点介词短语 `near the railway station` 全部移至各自被修饰名词的右侧。

143. **错误类型：原因句式语义与连词冗余叠加（`the reason why... because...`）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The company did not explain why the shipment had been delayed.`**（或 **`The company did not explain the reason why ( / that) the shipment had been delayed.`**）。  
     *解析*：`the reason why` 中关系副词 `why` 已经引导定语从句修饰 `reason`，其后绝不可再叠加原因连词 `because`。

#### 5.9.5.2 第 144—151 题（句法红线纠错第二组）

144. **错误类型：关系信号灯多余叠加（红线 5：`Although... but...` 冲突）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`Although the survey included more than five thousand households, its rural sample remained too small.`**（或 **`The survey included more than five thousand households, yet ( / but) its rural sample remained too small.`**）。  
     *解析*：删去 `but` 保留从属连词 `Although`，或删去 `Although` 保留并列转折连词 `yet / but`。

145. **错误类型：悬空修饰语（红线 2：Dangling Modifier）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`After reviewing the final report, the auditor discovered several inconsistencies.`**  
     *解析*：句首 `After reviewing the final report` 的逻辑主语必须是审阅报告的人（`the auditor`），故将主句由被动语态改为主动语态，使主句主语 `the auditor` 与分词逻辑主语严格对齐。

146. **错误类型：远距离主谓单复数脱节（红线 3）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The availability of affordable childcare services in these rapidly growing neighborhoods has influenced many parents' employment decisions.`**  
     *解析*：穿透两层介词短语 `of affordable childcare services` 与 `in these rapidly growing neighborhoods`，锁定句首单数不可数中心词 **`availability`**，将复数助动词 `have` 修正为单数 **`has`**。

147. **错误类型：非限制性定语从句误用 `that`（红线 6）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The laboratory replaced the faulty sensor, which had produced inaccurate readings for several weeks.`**  
     *解析*：逗号后对特定先行词 `the faulty sensor` 作补充说明的非限制性定语从句，关系代词必须将 `that` 修正为 **`which`**。

148. **错误类型：嵌入式一般疑问从句语序未还原（红线 7）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The committee asked whether the public consultation should be extended.`**  
     *解析*：`whether` 引导的宾语从句嵌入 `asked` 后必须恢复陈述语序，将情态助动词 `should` 放回主语 `the public consultation` 之后。

149. **错误类型：`There be` 存在句后直接堆叠限定谓语动词（双谓语冲突）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`There were several witnesses who claimed that the warning lights had failed.`**（或直接改写为主动主谓句：**`Several witnesses claimed that the warning lights had failed.`**）。  
     *解析*：在 `There were several witnesses` 后补入关系代词 `who` 将 `claimed...` 降级为定语从句，或直接以 `Several witnesses` 作全句主语。

150. **错误类型：中式长前置修饰堆叠（红线 1）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The housing assistance program recently proposed for low-income families near the city center has attracted criticism.`**  
     *解析*：先亮出核心复合名词主语 `The housing assistance program`，再将长修饰语 `recently proposed for low-income families near the city center` 整体置于中心词右侧作后置定语。

151. **错误类型：原因句式语义与连词冗余叠加（`the reason why... because...`）**。  
     *正确改写*：**`The report explains why several villages were excluded from the original survey.`**（或 **`The report explains the reason (why / that) several villages were excluded from the original survey.`**）。  
     *解析*：删去多余叠加的 `because`，直接用 `explains why...` 或 `explains the reason why...`。
